# MPCA ERP — Product Requirements Document

> **Madhya Pradesh Cricket Association — Enterprise Resource Planning System**
> Reference plan: https://mpca-plan-updated.netlify.app/
> Started: Jan 2026 · Last update: Feb 2026 — MPCA-242 Wiring-driven squad approval (kill MPCA approval + notifications for non-Mandatory types)

## Recent Changelog
- **Feb 2026 — MPCA-242 · Full wiring conformance for squad submission**: The wiring said `squad_approval.flag != "M"` for Inter-District (and 5 other types) but the code was still routing those submissions through `Awaiting_MPCA_Approval`, firing MPCA notifications, and showing "Submit to MPCA" copy. Systematic wiring-conformance pass instead of another point-fix. **(A) Backend** — (i) Broadened MPCA-239 auto-approve trigger in both submit endpoints (`POST /tournaments/{tid}/selection/submit` + `POST /squads/{sid}/submit`) from `flag == "NA"` to `flag != "M"`; Optional and NA both auto-approve now, only Mandatory routes to MPCA review. (ii) `POST /squads/{sid}/notify-ai-review` now short-circuits when wiring flag != "M" — returns `{notified: False, skipped: "wiring_squad_approval_not_mandatory", flag}` and does not create any MPCA notification. (iii) **Startup migration** `heal_legacy_stuck_squads()` runs on every boot (idempotent): scans `squads` for rows stuck at `Awaiting_MPCA_Approval` whose tournament wiring says flag != M and auto-flips them to Approved with `reviewed_by="auto-wiring-migration"`. Healed 1 pre-existing stuck squad (Indore District, Ujjain Championship). **(B) Frontend** — (i) `SquadDetail.jsx` now fetches `wiringApprovalFlag` from wiring-status. Submit button label conditional: "Submit to MPCA" for M, **"Lock Squad"** for O/NA. Prompt copy conditional ("note for MPCA reviewer" vs "internal note"). Post-submit alert copy conditional ("Submitted to MPCA for review" vs "Squad locked. No MPCA approval is required for this tournament type per the wiring"). (ii) `SelectionConsole.jsx` — same flag threading; submit button + post-submit alert conditional. New `mpca-observer-mode-banner` shown to MPCA persona on non-M tournaments (replaces the misleading "MPCA reviews it" banner) explicitly stating "MPCA does not approve — the participating body locks it locally." (iii) `TournamentSquadsPanel.jsx` — reads flag; section header + counts flip based on flag ("submitted/approved" vs "locked/final"). New status label "LOCKED" for Approved cards on non-M types so users don't infer MPCA approval happened. **(C) Testing** — new `test_mpca242_no_mpca_notify_for_na_approval.py` with 3 pytests: (1) locks the wiring contract that only `interdiv` may have `squad_approval.flag == "M"`, (2) verifies `notify-ai-review` short-circuits on non-M types, (3) verifies startup migration healed all stuck legacy squads. All 62 wiring-related pytests GREEN (+3 new). Frontend + backend lint clean. **Design intent recorded**: from this sprint forward, any downstream module that touches squad/officials/calendar/budget MUST read from wiring — hardcoded `isMPCA` / `isBCCI` / `tournament_type` role checks are considered wiring violations. The wiring config is the single source of truth for governance.

- **Feb 2026 — MPCA-241 · Fade register-picker for Manual_PDF squad wiring types**: The 2-column register-picker + selected-squad layout on `/squads/:sid` was fully rendered for all 8 tournament types even though the wiring says 6 of them (BCCI, Inter-District, Inter-School, Inter-Club, Coaching Camp, Vacation Camp) accept only a Manual PDF as the official squad list. Users viewing an Inter-District squad saw 17 test players in the pool + 15 already "nominated", which contradicted the wiring intent ("Districts don't run register-based selection — Division uploads a signed PDF per District"). **Light-touch fix** — `SquadDetail.jsx` now reads `wiringSquadMode.mode` (already fetched from `/tournaments/{tid}/wiring-status`) and when it equals `Manual_PDF`: (a) prepends a compact info ribbon above the 2-column body ("Picker below is optional · Manual PDF upload is the accepted flow for this tournament type"), (b) applies `opacity-50` to the entire 2-column grid with a hover-restore transition so users can still interact if they want to keep internal records, (c) firms up the existing advisory banner copy to explicitly state "this tournament type does not use the player register." No structural layout changes — the picker stays interactive but visually recedes. Register-linked types (Inter-Division + Pre-Tournament Camp) are unaffected. **Testing**: new `tests/test_mpca241_squad_picker_fade.py` with 2 pytests locking the wiring contract for all 8 types (Register_Linked for `interdiv` + `camp`, Manual_PDF for the other 6). All 29 wiring-adjacent pytests remain GREEN. Backend contract unchanged — pure frontend conditional rendering.

- **Feb 2026 — MPCA-236 · E2E verification of Tournament Wiring across all 8 types**: Critical bug fixes + comprehensive testing coverage. **(1) Type resolver bug fix** — `_resolve_type_id` in `tournament_wiring_status.py` was falling back to `interdiv` for 4 of 7 seeded tournaments (Inter-School, Inter-Club, Coaching Camp, Vacation Camp) because their `tournament_type_code` slugs (e.g. "inter_school", "vacation_camp") didn't match any Master Registry row. Added `_CODE_TO_TYPE` slug-prefix map so slugs resolve correctly. All 7 tournaments now map: 001→bcci, 002→interdiv, 003→district, 004→interschool, 005→interclub, 006→coachingcamp, 007→vacationcamp. **(2) Seed enrichment** — new `scripts/enrich_seed_data.py` adds 15 real player members to every empty squad (296 players across 12 squads = 180 members enriched) and calls the unified-budget compute-and-save endpoint per tournament (4 successful, 3 failed due to missing rate cards for schemes 2-A/2-D/2-B — non-blocking for wiring test). **(3) Reset audit trail** — `POST /tournament-wiring/reset` now writes a synthetic audit row (`type_id: "*", step_key: "*", diff: {"__reset__": [...]}`) so wholesale reversions are traceable in the Audit Log. **(4) Testing coverage** — testing agent added `test_mpca_235_wiring_e2e.py` with 30 new pytests. Total wiring epic pytest coverage now **48/48 GREEN** covering: matrix shape, PATCH validation + version bump + audit write-through, type_id resolver correctness for all 7 tournaments, live status derivation, Ship 4 visibility filter behaviour with seeded Championship-scope data (verified only 3 of 7 tournaments are visible to State persona by default, all 7 with include_camp_scoped=true), freeze-season snapshot revision incrementing + immutability against subsequent wiring edits, Inter-District tournament creation via API with corrected `MPCA_Championship` type. **Code review notes** (non-blocking): dict-ordering in `_CODE_TO_TYPE` currently safe (Python 3.7+ preserves insertion order), Ship 4 filter is in-memory (Python) rather than pushed into Mongo — acceptable at current scale (<200 tournaments). Zero regression on prior 25-pytest baseline.

## Recent Changelog: Fixed the `tournament_type` literal-mismatch bug that prevented Inter-District tournament creation — `tournamentCatalog.js` mapped Inter-District to `family: "Inter_District"` but the backend `TournamentType` enum only accepts `MPCA_InterDivisional / MPCA_Championship / BCCI / Invitational / Other`. Corrected to `family: "MPCA_Championship"` (scope stays `Inter_District`). New idempotent seed script `scripts/seed_wiring_e2e.py` wipes 17 tournament-related collections and reseeds **7 richly-populated tournaments** covering all 8 wiring types: (1) Ranji Trophy Elite [BCCI], (2) MY Memorial Trophy [Inter-Divisional with 2 pools of 5 divisions each, 5 fixtures, 4 MPCA-posted officials, 4 division squads], (3) Bhopal Pre-Tournament Camp [linked to MY Memorial as camp record], (4) Indore Inter-District Championship [MPCA_Championship + Inter_District scope, 2 pools of 4 districts each, 5 fixtures, 3 division-posted officials, 3 district squads], (5) Bhopal Inter-School Tournament [Championship scope, no pools/officials, division-owned squad], (6) Indore Inter-Club A-Grade Tournament [Championship scope, two-day KO format], (7) Ujjain Coaching Camp [3-A scheme, 15-day rural coaching camp], (8) Jabalpur Vacation Camp [3-B scheme, 21-day U-14 summer camp]. All Draft/Upcoming — nothing submitted so user can drive the wiring flow end-to-end. Ship 4 visibility filter working as designed: MPCA state persona sees only 3 core tournaments (BCCI + Inter-Div + Inter-District) by default with "4 hidden until Finance claim is submitted · Show all" chip. Fixed seed missing `team_name` on squad rows.

## Recent Changelog: The wiring epic's governance polish sprint. **Ship A · Setup-box reorder on Tournament Detail** — the 4×3 setup-box grid was in an arbitrary order (Match Officials tucked at the bottom). Reordered to follow the 9-step wiring progression: Tournament Basics → Participants Matrix → **Match Officials** (moved from bottom) → Squads → Match Calendar → Days Engine → Unified Budget → Finance Console (card-style) → Closure Letter → Activity Log · Discussion · Pre-Camps (utility footer). This makes the visual layout match the Progression Ribbon dot-order left-to-right and dramatically improves onboarding clarity for office bearers. **Ship B · Wiring Audit Log** — every `PATCH /tournament-wiring/cell` now writes an audit row to `tournament_wiring_audit` with `{id, type_id, step_key, diff, before, after, version, changed_by, changed_at}`. Diff computed field-by-field so the log shows exactly what flipped (e.g. `flag: M → O`, `text: "Yes — MPCA approves" → "Audit trail test"`). New endpoints: `GET /tournament-wiring/audit?limit=200&type_id=…&step_key=…` (chronological, filterable). New **Audit Log** tab in the Wiring Console UI with count badge, chronological table showing `When · Type · Step · Changed (diff pills) · By · Wiring v`. Refreshes live after every cell edit. **Ship B · Season Freeze** — new endpoint `POST /tournament-wiring/freeze-season/{cycle}` snapshots the current matrix into `tournament_wiring_snapshots` (versioned, immutable). Multiple snapshots per season allowed (revisions). New endpoints: `GET /tournament-wiring/snapshots?cycle=…`, `GET /tournament-wiring/snapshots/{id}` for JSON download. New **Snapshots** tab in the Wiring Console with per-season card (rev number, wiring version, frozen-at timestamp, frozen-by, Download JSON button). New "Freeze season" button in header (dark oxblood, only visible to MPCA state personas). **Ship C · Pre-Tournament Camp auto-link** — verified already complete via MPCA-204 (earlier sprint). Backend enforces `inter_division_tournament_id` on `POST /camps` for `Pre_Tournament_Camp` type (422 without, 404 if invalid, scope-check for Inter_Divisional, idempotent on tournament+body). Frontend `Camps.jsx` shows a required dropdown filtered to Inter-Div tournaments. No new work needed. **Testing**: 18/18 pytests GREEN across the epic (`test_tournament_wiring.py` 7, `test_tournament_wiring_status.py` 4, `test_ships_3_4_6.py` 3, `test_ships_a_b.py` 4). Playwright verified live: setup boxes render in wiring order (basics → participants → officials → squads → …), Audit Log tab shows the recorded edit with red strikethrough + green after values, Snapshots tab shows the frozen Season 2026-27 rev 1 card.

## Recent Changelog: Three complementary shipments layered on the wiring config from Ships 1/2 — every step remains fully clickable, no feature is disabled. **Ship 3 · Setup-box flag chips**: `SetupBox` in `TournamentDetail.jsx` now accepts a `flag` prop and renders a top-right pill — `REQUIRED` (saffron border) for Mandatory, `OPTIONAL` (brass) for Optional, `OPTIONAL·NOT USED` (dashed gray, per user directive) for NA, `INFO` for informational. NA boxes stay fully functional — the pill just tells the user their data won't drive downstream calculations for this tournament type. Tournament Detail fetches `/api/tournaments/{tid}/wiring-status` once on mount and builds a `boxTestId → flag` map covering 9 setup boxes (basics/participants/officials/squads/calendar/days-engine/unified-budget/finance/my-da). **Ship 4 · MPCA state-list visibility filter**: `GET /api/tournaments` gained an `include_camp_scoped: bool = True` query param. When explicitly `false` and caller is State scope, the endpoint filters out `Championship`/`Invitational`-scoped tournaments unless they have a submitted reimbursement claim (`Submitted/Under_Review/Approved/Rejected/Disbursed`). Backward-compatible — default is unchanged. Frontend `Tournaments.jsx` for State personas fetches both filtered + full and shows an unobtrusive `mpca-visibility-toggle` chip: "N tournaments hidden until their Finance claim is submitted · Show all" — click to flip. Chip hides itself when nothing is filtered. **Ship 6 · Squad-mode wiring banner**: `SquadDetail.jsx` reads the wiring `squad` step's `mode` field on mount. Renders an advisory banner between the workflow-guidance banner and the review actions: for `Manual_PDF` types (School/Club/Camp/Coaching/Vacation) shows "**Manual · PDF only** — the recommended flow is to upload a signed squad-list PDF" with parchment tone; for `Register_Linked` types (Inter-Div) shows "**Register-linked** — select players from your Player Register below" with ivory tone. Owner (Division/District/MPCA) surfaced in the banner header. Both PDF upload and player picker remain fully available in every mode — banner is advisory, never blocking. **Palette**: strictly MPCA tokens only (parchment, ivory, brass, oxblood, green-dark, gray-dark) — no external colours anywhere. **Testing**: 14/14 pytests GREEN across `test_tournament_wiring.py` (7) + `test_tournament_wiring_status.py` (4) + new `test_ships_3_4_6.py` (3 · include_camp_scoped backward-compat + filter behaviour + squad-mode enum surface). Playwright verified live: Parmanand Trophy detail shows 7 REQUIRED chips on setup boxes; SquadDetail (Register-linked) shows the "Wired for this tournament type · Division owned · Register-linked" banner as expected. Zero regression on the 25-pytest baseline suite.

## Recent Changelog: Live 9-step status ribbon that reads the wiring config + tournament state and renders on every tournament — advisory, non-blocking, keeps every step clickable. **(A) Backend** — new `routes/tournament_wiring_status.py` with `GET /api/tournaments/{tid}/wiring-status`. Resolves tournament→wiring type via Master Registry lookup (falls back to scope-based mapping). Derives 9 step statuses from live state: `tournament_creation` (always done once row exists), `pool_basics` (setup_meta.pools present), `match_official_posting` (tournament_match_officials count > 0), `squad` (any squad has members), `squad_approval` (all squads with members = Approved), `match_calendar` (tournament_matches count > 0), `unified_budget` (snapshot locked), `finance_console` (claim submitted → approved → paid). Each step returns status ∈ {done, current, pending, na, info} + a short note (e.g. "4 fixtures", "Approved · awaiting UTR", "Realtime to MPCA"). First pending Mandatory step is auto-marked `current` for the pulse highlight. Also computes `progress_pct` (done Mandatory ÷ total Mandatory). Each step carries an `anchor` field pointing to the `data-testid` of its setup box for smooth-scroll on click. **(B) Frontend** — new `components/TournamentProgressionRibbon.jsx` with TWO exports: (i) default `<TournamentProgressionRibbon />` — full ribbon with 9 numbered/checked circles connected by tinted lines, bucket labels (Pre-Tournament / In-Tournament / Post-Tournament), per-step flag chip (M/O/NA/INFO) + owner chip + note tooltip + legend footer, progress % + wiring version chip in header. (ii) `<TournamentProgressionRibbonMini />` — compact 9-dot strip for the Tournaments list row (client-side derivation from list payload, zero extra API calls per row). **Palette**: strictly MPCA tokens only — navy (mpca-green-dark) for Done, saffron (mpca-oxblood) for Current with pulse, parchment for Pending, dashed ivory for NA, brass tint for Info. No colours introduced outside the ERP design system. **(C) Wiring** — mounted into `TournamentDetail.jsx` header (below existing `TournamentProgress`) and into every row of `Tournaments.jsx` list (below the meta line, above the pills). Every dot has `data-testid=wiring-dot-{key}` and clicking scrolls to the setup box using `data-testid=box-*` anchors already on the page. **(D) Testing** — `tests/test_tournament_wiring_status.py` with 4/4 pytests GREEN (shape, creation-always-done invariant, current-marker-present-or-all-done invariant, unknown-tournament 404). Playwright verified live: Parmanand Trophy on preview shows 12% complete · Wiring v6 · Tournament Creation ✓ · Pool Basics ● (current, pulsing) · steps 3-8 pending · MPCA Visibility ⓘ · legend + advisory caption rendered correctly. Zero regression on existing 25-pytest suite.

## Recent Changelog
- **Feb 2026 — MPCA-235 · Ship 1 · Tournament Wiring Console (Governance Layer)**: New single-source-of-truth for the tournament progression matrix — 9 steps × 8 tournament types × 8 attributes per cell, seeded verbatim from the user-uploaded `MPCA_Tournament_Wiring_TwoTab.html` artifact. **(A) Backend** — new `routes/tournament_wiring.py` with a `tournament_wiring` singleton collection (~30 KB total; loaded once, cached client-side). Endpoints: `GET /api/tournament-wiring` (returns matrix + enum options), `PATCH /cell` (updates one cell + bumps version + validates all enums server-side), `POST /reset` (restores defaults), `GET /export` (portable JSON for signed archives). Idempotent seeder wired into `server.py` lifespan. **8 configurable attributes per cell**: `flag` (M/O/NA/INFO), `owner` (MPCA/Division/District/Auto), `approver` (MPCA/Division/None), `mode` (Register_Linked/Manual_PDF/Auto_Compute/NA), `visibility` (Realtime/On_Submit/Never), `blocks_next` (bool), `sla_days` (int|null), `text` (free-form). 72 cells seeded with faithful defaults matching the HTML artifact. **(B) Frontend** — new page `TournamentWiringConsole.jsx` at `/tournament-wiring` (MPCA-only edit, read-only for other personas). Two tabs matching the HTML artifact: **Tab 1 · Wiring Matrix** (grid with steps as rows, types as columns, colour-coded flag chips, owner chips, mode/SLA/gates-next meta pills, click any cell to edit); **Tab 2 · By Type** (per-type card view with the full 9-step pipeline read-out). Cell editor is a modal with all 8 attributes (dropdowns for enums, number input for SLA, textarea for text). Header shows persistent version chip, Export JSON + Reset-to-defaults buttons. Sidebar link added under Operations. **(C) Testing** — `tests/test_tournament_wiring.py` with 7/7 pytests GREEN. **Design intent**: pure governance/visibility layer — ZERO impact on existing tournament runtime code.

## Recent Changelog
- **Feb 2026 — MPCA-234 · Phase B+C · Match Official Finance Page + MPCA Review Flow**: Delivered the full match-official financial workflow as a dedicated per-tournament page + a signed-scan gated MPCA review flow with deductions and payment. **(B1 Model)** extended `MatchOfficialDA` with `official_signed_claim_url`, `mpca_signed_review_url`, and `mpca_deductions: List[Dict]` (id/head/amount/reason/added_by/added_at). **(B2 Backend)** — 5 new endpoints in `routes/tournament_plan.py`: (i) `GET /tournaments/{tid}/my-finance-page` (consolidated payload for Match Official — assignment snapshot with Fee/DA rate-card allocation, DA form, 6-stage progress derivation, tournament summary); (ii) `POST /match-official-da/{did}/official-signed-scan` (Official uploads signed draft PDF — required before Submit); (iii) `POST /match-official-da/{did}/mpca-signed-scan` (MPCA uploads signed review PDF — required before Approve); (iv) `POST /match-official-da/{did}/deductions` + (v) `DELETE …/deductions/{id}` (per-head deductions with reason). Also gated `submit` on `official_signed_claim_url` presence, gated `approve` on `mpca_signed_review_url`, and **approve now recomputes `total_inr = total_claimed - sum(deductions)`** before flipping to Approved. Also relaxed `self-create` allocation check to accept `tournament_match_officials` OR legacy `squads.match_officials`. **(B3 Frontend — new page `MatchOfficialFinancePage.jsx` @ `/my-finance/:tid`)** — dedicated Match Official workspace with: (a) 6-stage progress bar (Budget Allocated → Tournament Running → Tournament Completed → Claim Submitted → MPCA Approved → MPCA Paid); active stage pulses oxblood, completed stages fill green with checkmark, connector lines colour-code between stages. Progress derivation: Budget Allocated = `acceptance_status == Accepted`; **Tournament Running = `unified_budget_snapshot.is_locked` (per user directive)**; Tournament Completed = tournament status Completed OR played_days > 0; Claim/Approved/Paid follow DA form status. (b) 3-tile Budget Allocated card (Match Fee scheduled · DA played · MPCA-Allocated Total) with per-day rate breakdown + provisional badge when budget not yet locked. (c) Step-1 DA/TA form (embeds existing `MatchOfficialDAPanel`). (d) Step-2 "Sign & Submit" block — 3 columns: Download Draft PDF (→ voucher route) · Upload Signed Scan (file input, turns green with checkmark on success) · Submit to MPCA (disabled unless signed scan present + total_inr > 0). (e) Post-submit lifecycle blocks: Under Review · Approved · Payment Received (with UTR + Landmark icon + final voucher download). Also updated `MyAssignments.jsx` and `TournamentDetail.jsx` (My DA/TA setup box) to route the match-official CTA to `/my-finance/{tid}` instead of the modal-style DA panel. **(C Frontend — Finance Console TA/DA Review Modal)** — added Review button on Submitted rows in `FinanceMatchOfficialsDAPaymentsPanel.jsx`. Modal shows: (i) 8-cell claimed breakup grid + Official's signed scan link, (ii) MPCA Deductions table with inline add form (Head dropdown / Amount / Reason / Add) + net approvable footer showing live "₹Total − ₹Deductions", (iii) MPCA Signed Review PDF section (Download voucher link + Upload scan input, turns green when uploaded), (iv) footer with "Send Back to Official" and "Approve Claim" — approve disabled until `mpca_signed_review_url` present. **Verified live end-to-end**: DA-2026-27-0004 filled ₹5,000 → uploaded Official scan → Submitted → MPCA added ₹200 deduction (Travel Fare · "Boarding pass missing") → tried Approve without MPCA scan (**400**: "Please download the MPCA review PDF, sign it, and upload the scan before approving.") → uploaded MPCA scan → Approve succeeded, `total_inr` recomputed **5000 → 4800** (deduction applied). Then Treasurer marked Paid via the existing UTR modal. Match Official's Finance Page renders 6-stage progress bar with correct current stage (Tournament Running highlighted since budget is locked). 26 disabled inputs / 0 editable on Tournament Basics confirms the read-only guard for match-official persona.


## Recent Changelog
- **Feb 2026 — MPCA-234 · Phase A · Match Official Portal Polish + Read-Only Guard**: Three quick fixes to unblock the finance-portal redesign. **(A1) DA self-create for centrally-assigned officials** — `POST /match-official-da/self-create` was checking only `squads.match_officials` (the legacy Division-nominated block) so centrally assigned umpires (via `tournament_match_officials`) got a blanket 403. Updated the allocation check in `routes/tournament_plan.py` to accept EITHER a `tournament_match_officials` hit OR a squad row; central assignment takes priority since it's the modern MPCA-133 flow. Error copy improved from "please contact your Division/MPCA" to "please contact MPCA to be assigned first." **Verified live**: Chandrakant Pandit (only present in `tournament_match_officials`, not in any squad) can now open the DA panel and auto-materialise a Draft form (`DA-2026-27-0004`) on the Madhavrao Scindia Trophy — previously returned "You are not allocated to this tournament." **(A2) Tournament Basics + Match Calendar locked for Match Officials** — `TournamentDetail.jsx` now computes `isMatchOfficial = persona.id === 'match-official' || persona.body_type === 'Match_Official'` and threads a new `canEditSetup = canEdit && !isMatchOfficial` prop into `TournamentBasicsPanel` and `MatchCalendarPanel`. Officials still see the same tabs (they need to know pool/date info) but every input is `disabled`. Confirmed 26 disabled inputs / 0 editable inputs on the Basics panel when the umpire persona opens `/tournaments/{tid}`. **(A3) Filter chips + search on My Assignments** — `MyAssignments.jsx` now renders 6 filter chips (`All / Awaiting Response / Accepted / Approved / Paid / Rejected`) above the assignment table with live counts, plus a 260px search box (`ma-search`, `Search` icon) that filters by tournament name / role / DA ref. Filter/search are combined with `useMemo`. Empty-filter state renders an inline "Clear filters" link. Each chip has a stable `data-testid` (`ma-filter-{key}`). Verified live: Paid chip narrows to 1 row (Chandrakant × MY Memorial), search "Madhavrao" narrows to 1 row across all statuses.


## Recent Changelog
- **Feb 2026 — MPCA-233 · Match Officials Payments Portal + TA/DA Voucher PDF**: End-to-end payment visibility for match officials — from Approve → Mark Paid (Treasurer) → PAID stamp on voucher → UTR chip on the official's portal. **(A) Model** — extended `MatchOfficialDA` with 6 payment fields: `paid_at`, `paid_amount_inr`, `payment_ref` (UTR/cheque/UPI txn id), `payment_mode` (NEFT/UPI/RTGS/Cheque/Cash), `payment_notes`, `paid_by`. **(B) Backend** — 3 new endpoints in `tournament_plan.py`: (i) `POST /match-official-da/{did}/mark-paid` (Treasurer records UTR + mode + date + notes; guards status ∈ {Approved, Paid}; fires in-app notification to the official); (ii) `POST /match-official-da/{did}/mark-unpaid` (reversal — rolls status back to Approved, clears payment fields); (iii) `GET /tournaments/{tid}/match-official-payments` (aggregate view for Finance Console — returns per-form fee/DA/travel/misc split + payment info + assignment linkage + rollup {submitted, approved, paid, count, total_approved}). Also enhanced `GET /match-officials/me/assignments` to batch-fetch DA forms by (tournament_id, role) and enrich each assignment row with `da_form_id`, `da_ref`, `da_form_status`, `da_total_claim_inr`, `da_paid_amount_inr`, `da_payment_ref`, `da_payment_mode`, `da_paid_at`, `da_rejection_reason`. **(C) Frontend — Match Official portal** (`MyAssignments.jsx`): KPI strip expanded from 4 → 5 tiles (Total Assignments · Awaiting Response · Accepted · **Approved · Awaiting Payment** · **Total Paid**). Table now shows 10 columns including new **DA Form** status pill (Not Started / Draft / Submitted / Approved / Rejected / Paid) and **Payment** column (₹ amount + Landmark icon + mode + UTR + date). Actions column adapts to lifecycle: `Accept/Reject` (Pending) → `Fill DA Form` (Accepted, no DA) → `Continue` (Draft/Rejected) → `View` (Submitted) → `Voucher` (Approved/Paid). **(D) Frontend — Voucher PDF** (`MatchOfficialDAVoucher.jsx` at route `/match-official-da/{did}/voucher`): MPCA letterhead (Holkar Stadium address, BCCI affiliation), Form ref `FMPCA · MO-037 · Payment Voucher`, meta grid (voucher ref, official, role, association, place, purpose, approved-on), head-wise breakup table (Match Fee / DA / Travel / Journey / Conveyance / Incidental / Night Halt / Misc — filters out zero rows, shows `days × rate` basis), Grand Total. If Paid: green 12°-rotated PAID watermark + full "Payment Recorded" block (paid amount, mode, UTR, paid-on, recorded-by, notes). Beneficiary bank details section + 3 signature blocks (Treasurer · Secretary · Received by). **(E) Frontend — Finance Console TA/DA tab** (`FinanceMatchOfficialsDAPaymentsPanel.jsx`): replaces the raw DA form editor in the `da` tab with a payments-first listing — 6 rollup tiles (Forms / Fee / DA / Travel+Misc / Approved / Paid), 10-column table (Official / Role / Days S÷P / Fee / DA / Travel / Total / Status / Payment / Actions). Approved rows show `Mark Paid` button (opens modal with Mode + UTR + Amount + Date + Notes); Paid rows show `Reverse` button. Voucher link on both Approved and Paid rows. Legacy single-form editor accessible via a `<details>` toggle. **Verified live** (curl + Playwright): full lifecycle Draft → Submitted → Approved → **Paid ₹8,000 via NEFT UTRN2026021512345** for Chandrakant Pandit's Umpire assignment on MY Memorial Trophy → Match Official portal shows KPI Total Paid=₹8,000 + Paid pill + Payment chip + Voucher button → Voucher route renders MPCA letterhead with green PAID stamp, head breakup (Match Fee ₹3,600 + DA ₹2,400 + Travel ₹2,000 = ₹8,000), Payment Recorded block with UTR/mode/date. Guards verified: `mark-paid` returns 409 on Draft forms, 422 on missing ref; reverse rolls back cleanly to Approved with cleared payment fields.


- **Feb 2026 — MPCA-232 · Master Rate Card · Match Officials Rates Panel**: Officials' pay rates now live at the **season × format** level (not manually re-typed for every assignment). **(A) Model** — added `officials_rates: Dict[str, {fee_per_day, da_per_day}]` to `RateCard` (roles: Umpire · Scorer · Selector · Observer · Referee — Observer & Referee share a single UI row that writes to both keys) and `officials_rates` slot to `RateCardPatch`. **(B) Backend** — `_DEFAULT_OFFICIALS_RATES` seed constants for both format groups (ltd_overs: Umpire 700/500, Scorer 500/400, Selector 800/600, Observer/Referee 1500/700; multi_day: Umpire 1000/700, Scorer 700/500, Selector 1000/700, Observer/Referee 2000/900). `GET /rate-cards/for/…` auto-backfills legacy cards missing this field. `PATCH /rate-cards/{id}` sanitises incoming rates (drops unknown roles, clamps to non-negative floats). `POST /rate-cards/reset/{id}` restores officials_rates alongside budget/travel rates. `POST /tournaments/{tid}/match-officials` (assignment endpoint) now prefers the tournament's active Master Rate Card's officials_rates over the legacy `STANDARD_MO_FEES_INR/STANDARD_MO_DA_INR` constants — explicit MPCA overrides still win. **(C) Frontend** — new `Match Officials Rates · per role` section in `/rate-cards` (RateCardMaster.jsx), rendered between Custom Line Items and Travel-Grant tables. Navy banner with Gavel icon, 4-row editable table (Umpire / Scorer / Selector / Observer/Referee) × 2 columns (Fee/day + DA/day), footer caption "Fee is paid on scheduled days. DA is paid on actual days played. Existing assignments keep their snapshot rates — new assignments will inherit these values." Save button in the sticky action bar now includes officials_rates in the PATCH payload. **Verified live**: switching format_group from multi_day (Umpire 1000/700) to ltd_overs (Umpire 700) reloads correct rates. PATCH round-trip Umpire→1200/800 persisted, then confirmed `POST /tournaments/{tid}/match-officials` for Ashok Mehta / Umpire / 3 days materialised with `per_day_fee_inr=1200, per_day_da_inr=800` (drawn from rate card, not the STANDARD 700/500 fallback). Reset restored 1000/700. Non-editable in the Unified Budget Officials sub-tab — this is now the single source of truth. Frontend + backend smoke-tested via curl + Playwright.

- **Feb 2026 — MPCA-226 · Deprecate Legacy Schemes + Locked Budget Watermark**: Two P1 wins in a single sprint. **(A) Legacy scheme deprecation** — Finance Console `Prepare Budgets` now routes to the Unified Budget engine for tournament scopes `{Inter_Divisional, Inter_District, BCCI, Championship, Pre_Tournament_Camp}`; legacy `scheme_calc` (2-B / 2-D) stays live only for `{Inter_School, Inter_Club, Invitational}` etc. **Model** — extended compute engine's `by_body_totals` to include per-row `head_allocations: [{head_key, head, owner, limit_inr}]` (owner-attributed head-by-head breakdown so downstream can materialise `TournamentBudget` docs). Travel Grant merged as a synthetic head_allocation on visiting-Division rows (owner=Visitor). **Backend** — new endpoint `POST /api/tournaments/{tid}/finance/prepare-budgets-unified` (MPCA-only): reads locked snapshot when frozen else runs a live compute+travel merge; creates one `TournamentBudget` Draft per body from `by_body_totals`; preserves live (Sent/Accepted/Approved) rows; replaces Draft/Revision_Requested rows; skips MPCA (state books, not a claimant); honours `per_body_head_overrides`. Guard: returns HTTP 400 when scope is not covered by the Unified engine. **Frontend** — `TournamentFinanceConsole.jsx` `prepareBudgets` handler routes to unified endpoint via `UNIFIED_SCOPES` set; persistent oxblood "UNIFIED BUDGET ENGINE" badge (`fc-unified-engine-badge`) + caption "Legacy scheme calculators (2-B / 2-D) are deprecated for this scope" pinned to the Budgets tab section header (shows `🔒 v{N}` when snapshot locked). **(B) Locked Budget Watermark** — reimbursement claim outputs now stamp the locked snapshot version + date so auditors can trace ceiling→claim. **Frontend**: (i) `ReimbursementClaims.jsx` fetches `/tournaments/{tid}` on claim-detail load; header renders `claim-locked-snapshot-chip` ("🔒 Locked Budget v{N} · {date}") when `unified_budget_snapshot.is_locked=true`. (ii) `DivisionReimbursementClaimForm.jsx` renders `reimb-locked-watermark` block ("Locked Snapshot · Budget v{N} · Frozen {date} · by MPCA · Claim ceiling frozen at this snapshot version") between Tournament Details and head-wise table. (iii) `MpcaClaimReviewForm.jsx` renders `mpca-locked-watermark` block below meta grid ("Locked Snapshot · Unified Budget v{N} · Frozen {date} · by MPCA · Sanctioned ceiling frozen at this snapshot; deductions apply against it"). **Verified live**: SM Khan Trophy `prepare-budgets-unified` created 2 drafts — Bhopal Division ₹1,31,690 (17 heads · Host) + Rewa Division ₹67,900 (1 head · Travel Grant / Visitor). MY Memorial Trophy locked to v7, linked claim (781d7979) rendered all three watermark artifacts correctly. **Testing** (`iteration_92.json`): backend 3/3 GREEN + frontend 7/7 GREEN (was 6/7 — post-fix badge assertion now passes on persistent placement). 25/25 pytests still green.

- **Feb 2026 — MPCA-225 · Unified Budget → Finance Console Freeze Workflow (COMPLETE)**: The freeze/lock workflow that ties the Unified Budget compute engine to the Finance Console proposed column. **(A) Model** — added `unified_budget_snapshot: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None` to `Tournament` model in `/app/backend/models.py` so the field round-trips via `GET /api/tournaments/{tid}` (previously stripped by Pydantic serialisation — frontend's on-mount hydration read `is_locked` as always undefined). **(B) Backend** — compute engine's `by_body_totals` owner-attributed rollup (per-Division / Host / MPCA) merges Travel Grants when snapshot is written. New endpoints `POST /api/tournaments/{tid}/unified-budget/lock` (freezes snapshot, bumps `locked_version`, stamps `locked_at` + `locked_by='MPCA'`) and `POST /api/tournaments/{tid}/unified-budget/unlock` (idempotent — returns `{already: unlocked}` when not locked). Also `GET /api/tournaments/{tid}/unified-budget/proposed/{body_code}` returns per-body proposed ceiling with `source: 'locked' | 'live'`. **(C) Frontend** — `UnifiedBudgetPanel.jsx`: new "Budget by Body" tab (`ub-tab-body`) with `ub-body-table` rendering Body / Budget ₹ / Travel Grant ₹ / Total ₹ / %. Lock/Unlock buttons (`ub-lock` / `ub-unlock`) in the action bar with confirm() prompts. Persistent oxblood-tinted Lock banner (`ub-lock-banner`) showing "Budget locked at v{N}" + localised timestamp. `ub-save-snapshot` disabled with title="Unlock first" while locked. On-mount hydration reads `tournament.unified_budget_snapshot.is_locked` to restore state across reloads. Also fixed React key warning by replacing shorthand `<>...</>` inside `matches.map()` with `<Fragment key={m.id}>`. **Verified live** on MY Memorial Trophy (8cf98196): 4 body rows populated correctly — DIV-BPL=₹15,23,860 (host budget) + DIV-GWL=₹95,900 + DIV-JBP=₹81,900 + DIV-RWA=₹81,900 (all visiting travel grants). Lock v4 → v5 round-trip successful; state persists across hard reload. **Testing** (`iteration_91.json`): 24/25 assertions GREEN via testing_agent — one LOW-priority note about ub-saved toast auto-dismiss timing (banner already proves lock success). 25/25 pytests still green.

- **Feb 2026 — MPCA-224 · Editable Head Metadata (Name / Driver / Owner / Basis / Rooms)**: MPCA can now rename any of the 17 default heads, swap its driver, change its owner tag, toggle the rooms rule, or flip basis MatchDays↔Match — for both the default 17 rows AND the custom line items. **(A) Model** — added `head_meta_overrides: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]` to `RateCard` (shape `{head_key: {name?, driver?, owner?, rooms?, basis?}}`). **(B) Backend** — new unified endpoint `PATCH /api/rate-cards/{card_id}/heads/{key}` that routes writes to `head_meta_overrides` for default keys and updates the `custom_heads` entry in-place for custom keys. Validates driver (must be one of 7 known values or null), owner (Host/Visitor/Officials/Common), basis (MatchDays/Match), rooms (bool), name (non-empty string). Compute engine merges `head_meta_overrides` on top of `BUDGET_HEADS_META` BEFORE iterating so every downstream (head_totals + per_head + owner rollup) reflects the change immediately. **(C) Frontend** — replaced the read-only Head / Driver / Owner cells with text-input / select / colour-tinted select in BOTH the "Tournament Rate Card · 17 heads" table AND the "Custom Line Items" table; Basis is also editable on custom rows. On-blur (name) and on-change (dropdowns) PATCH the head immediately. **Verified live**: PATCH `hotel_team` name→"Team Lodging" + owner→"Host" → head_meta_overrides stores it, compute engine reflects it in head_totals (`name="Team Lodging"`, `owner="Host"`, ₹4,05,000); reset restores originals. 25/25 pytests still green.

- **Feb 2026 — MPCA-223 · Rate-Card Custom Line Items + Officials-Wiring Verification**: MPCA can now extend any rate card with additional budget heads beyond the 17 defaults. **(A) Model** — added `custom_heads: List[Dict]` to `RateCard` (shape `{key, name, driver, rooms, basis, owner, md_rate, nmd_rate, is_custom}`) plus new `RateCardCustomHead` create-payload model. **(B) Backend** — new endpoints `POST /api/rate-cards/{card_id}/custom-heads` (auto-slugs a unique `custom_<slug>` key, injects rate into `budget_rates`) and `DELETE /api/rate-cards/{card_id}/custom-heads/{key}` (drops the head + its rate). Compute engine's iteration now uses `all_heads = BUDGET_HEADS_META + rate_card.custom_heads` so every match automatically gets custom heads in `per_head` output; head_totals include `is_custom` flag. **(C) Frontend** — new "Custom Line Items" section in `/rate-cards` (oxblood banner, count badge, empty state) with per-row driver / basis / owner / MD-rate / NMD-rate / delete columns. **+ Add Line Item** modal collects: name, driver dropdown (7 options: Flat / AwayTeamPax / HostTeamPax / MatchOfficialsPax / AllPax / TeamCount / HostTeamCount), basis (MatchDays vs once-per-Match), owner (Host/Visitor/Officials/Common), rooms-rule checkbox, MD + NMD rates. **Verified live**: POST'd "Trophy engraving" (once-per-match, ₹3,000, Common owner) → compute picked up 4 matches × ₹3,000 = ₹12,000 added to Common bucket; grand total bumped ₹15,23,860 → ₹15,35,860. DELETE cleanly removes. **(D) Officials-count wiring verified**: PATCH'd `officials_ids.umpires=[Chandrakant Pandit]` on a fixture → compute correctly reports `officials_count=1`, `hotel_off` driver qty=`ceil(1/2)=1 room`; days-engine endpoint also shows `officials_count=1`. Feature was already working — earlier "always 0" observations were because those specific fixtures had no `officials_ids` set. 25/25 pytests still green.

Compute engine's `derived_qty` prefers override when present; otherwise recomputes from formula. **Verified live**: League R1 (DIV-BPL v DIV-GWL, MD=2, NMD=2, ₹2,57,380 total) renders 17 head rows with exact formulas — Hotel-team `9 rooms auto · ₹1,800×9×2d=₹32,400 · ₹1,800×9×2d=₹32,400 · Total ₹64,800`; PATCH `driver_overrides={hotel_team:12}` flips qty 9→12, `is_override=true` (input styled with oxblood tint), Total ₹64,800→₹86,400. Plan for Finance Console linkage documented: extend compute to emit `by_body_totals` owner-attributed rollup → `sync_pipeline` prefers unified snapshot over legacy scheme value → Budget Freeze workflow locks snapshot at version N so Divisions claim against a fixed ceiling. 25/25 pytests still green.

Deleted the entire `bulletin-card p-4 mb-6` block plus the now-unused `commentText` state, `addComment` handler, and `MessageSquare`/`Send` icon imports. Backend `POST /reimbursement-claims/{cid}/comment` endpoint left intact for any legacy consumers. (2) **All budget heads selectable in deduction dropdown**: The MPCA "Add Deduction" form's Budget Head `<select>` was greying out heads with `spent <= 0` (via `disabled={spent <= 0}`), preventing MPCA from deducting against budgeted-but-unspent heads (e.g. reversing a head that Division never used but claimed in error). Removed the `disabled` attribute so every head from the submitted budget is selectable while retaining the descriptive label (`spent ₹X` vs `budget ₹Y · no spend yet`). Verified via Playwright: `Total heads: 10, Disabled: 0` on the live "Under Review" claim. No comments-section rendered.

- **Feb 2026 — MPCA-168 · Reimbursement Claim Deep-Dive (5 phases delivered)**: End-to-end reimbursement workflow rebuilt to match MPCA-168 spec. User choices confirmed: Division PDF on **Division** letterhead (not MPCA); MPCA writes **accepted** amount + reason (not reduction); sequential build. **Phase A backend** (`models.py`, `routes/reimbursement_claims.py`): New `MpcaInvoiceReview` model + `claim.mpca_invoice_reviews[]` field. Three new endpoints: `POST /reimbursement-claims/{cid}/invoice-review`, `DELETE …/invoice-review/{invoice_id}`, `GET …/review-summary` (returns per-head Budget / Spent-by-Division / Accepted-by-MPCA + per-invoice review lines with proration for multi-head allocations), `POST …/mpca-signed-pdf`. `approve` endpoint gated: when reviews exist, requires (1) every invoice reviewed, (2) MPCA-signed PDF uploaded — then computes `approved_amount_inr` as sum of accepted. Added belt-and-braces cap so sum-of-accepted ≤ eligible ceiling. **Phase B frontend** (`pages/DivisionReimbursementClaimForm.jsx`): New printable at `/reimbursement-claims/{id}/division-form` — Division letterhead (body name + seat, no MPCA emblem) with sections: Addressee → Subject → Tournament Details → Head-wise Sanctioned/Spent/Difference → Summary of Claim → 5-bullet Declaration → three Division signature blocks (Secretary/Treasurer/President). ClaimsPanel row for Division persona now shows a `Print PDF` button opening this route. **Phase C frontend** (`pages/ReimbursementClaims.jsx` · new `MpcaLineItemReviewPanel`): MPCA sees a new panel between Attached Invoices and Comments on Submitted/Under_Review claims. Panel shows the per-head Budget/Spent/Accepted table + each invoice row with `Accept full` (green) + `Partial` (oxblood) buttons. Partial opens an inline form with numeric `Accepted Amount` (capped at invoice total) + `Reason` free-text + Save/Cancel. `reset` clears a review. Live counter shows "X of Y invoices reviewed". Once all reviewed AND the MPCA-signed PDF is uploaded, the header Approve button unlocks (was disabled with tooltip explaining why). **Phase D frontend** (`pages/MpcaClaimReviewForm.jsx`): New printable at `/reimbursement-claims/{id}/mpca-review-form` — MPCA letterhead with real emblem, meta grid, Section A head-wise Budget/Spent/Accepted table, Section B per-invoice acceptance rows with reason column, MPCA Decision paragraph, three MPCA signature blocks (Reimbursement In-Charge/Treasurer/Secretary). Panel wires `Print MPCA Review PDF` link + `Upload signed PDF` file input (uses shared /api/uploads then POSTs /mpca-signed-pdf). **Phase E backend** (`routes/tournament_invoices.py`): Post-submission lock — once a reimbursement claim on (tournament, body) is Submitted/Under_Review/Approved, invoice CRUD returns HTTP 409 with the offending claim_ref in the message. Applies to CREATE, PATCH, DELETE — Division can't quietly retouch expenses after MPCA has already opened line-item review. Unlocks when the claim is Rejected. **Testing** (`iteration_90.json`): 20/20 pytest GREEN in `test_mpca_168_reimbursement.py`; frontend routes + testids all validated. Reviewer future nits: (a) proration divides by 1.0 when allocations sum to 0 (edge case), (b) District-claim invoices are also locked by Phase E which may need product confirmation, (c) 422 float formatting shows scientific notation for absurd values.

## Recent Changelog
- **Feb 2026 — Sprint Squad-Iter-B · Squad Selection Deep-Dive (4 tickets + 1 extra)**: User asked for the final squad tickets and an extra ask. Choices: free-text per-player reasons; approve-whole-list gated on all-decided; two PDFs (nomination + MPCA review); numeric age cap/floor as source of truth. **MPCA-140 · Per-Player Approve/Reject**: New `MemberDecision` model on Squad — `{player_id, decision:'Approved'|'Rejected', reason, decided_by, decided_at}`. New endpoints: `POST /api/squads/{sid}/members/{pid}/decision` (MPCA-only, requires Awaiting_MPCA_Approval, overwrites prior decision on same player) and `DELETE /api/squads/{sid}/members/{pid}/decision`. `review_squad_by_mpca` on `action=approve` now requires EVERY nominated member to have a decision (400 with count otherwise); rejected members are moved out of `members` into `dropped_members` at approve-time so the audit PDF can enumerate them. SquadDetail UI: each Selected Squad row gets green ✓ / red ✗ buttons + reset link in review mode. Approve button relabelled 'Approve Whole List', gated on `allMembersDecided`, shows an X/Y counter, tooltip explains why it's disabled. **MPCA-136 · Age Filter**: SquadDetail's player-pool `useMemo` now runs a `_seasonAgeCutoff` (1-Sept of the season start year) + `_ageOnDate` calculation and hides players where `age > age_cap_years` or `age < age_floor_years`. Null DOBs pass through as advisory. **MPCA-137 · Age Sync**: TournamentBasicsPanel's Age Group dropdown now runs through `setAgeGroup(label)` which sets `setup_meta.age_group = label` AND writes numeric `age_cap_years` on the Tournament (via `AGE_GROUP_TO_CAP` map: U-14→14, U-16→16, ..., U-25→25, Senior→null). Both persist in one save cycle. **MPCA-127 · Signable Nomination PDF (letterhead)**: SquadNominationForm gained an official MPCA letterhead — a circular MPCA/EST/1957 emblem placeholder, uppercase serif org name, affiliation line, Holkar Stadium address, and a double-line border-b separator. Signature block (Selection Committee Chairman + Hon. Secretary) untouched. **MPCA-142 · MPCA Review PDF**: NEW route `/squads/{sid}/mpca-review-form` at `/app/frontend/src/pages/SquadMPCAReviewForm.jsx`. Merges live `members` + archived `dropped_members`, annotates each with its `MemberDecision`, renders a table with Decision + Reason columns (APPROVED/REJECTED/PENDING pills), overall MPCA reviewer note, and THREE MPCA signature blocks (Chairman Selection Committee, Hon. Secretary, President). Link opened from SquadDetail via new 'MPCA Review PDF' button visible in review mode. Route registered in `App.js`. **Player Registration extra · Send back to Division**: MPCA persona sees a new `pr-return-to-division-btn` button labelled 'Send back to Division' on the review drawer for non-terminal registrations. Prompts for the missing-doc reason and calls the existing `/return-to-player` endpoint (which already accepted MPCA callers) with `remark=<reason>` — status flips to Returned, Division sees it in the Returned filter, existing amend modal + audit trail work unchanged. **Testing** (`iteration_89.json`): 11/11 backend pytest GREEN in `test_mpca_squad_iter_b.py` (1 skipped for lack of test data — endpoint code-verified), 100% frontend assertions across MPCA-140/142/127/137. Reviewer nits (future): (a) `return-to-player` endpoint carries no `return_type` field so audit trail can't distinguish Division-bounce vs Player-bounce; (b) age filter is client-side only — MPCA could theoretically bypass via API; (c) `AGE_GROUP_TO_CAP` map could be exported from a shared constants module.

Deleted the second box; the multi-body `Squads` box is now the single entry point. **MPCA-154 · No player-add from Tournament Detail**: TournamentDetail had a full inline "Participating Teams / Squads" grid with an "Add Player" button, a New-Squad dialog, and an Add-Player dialog — MPCA could add players directly bypassing the workflow. - **Feb 2026 — Sprint T-RIM · Multi-Head Invoice Set-Off + Approved-Budget Filtering**: User requested (a) one uploaded invoice can be split across multiple budget heads (e.g. combined hotel+food bill), and (b) the Budget Head dropdown must be restricted to the line-items actually present in the tournament's approved budget (not the full 11-code master list). **Backend** (`routes/tournament_invoices.py`): `TournamentInvoicePatch` gained `allocations: Optional[List[InvoiceHeadAllocation]]`; `POST /api/tournament-invoices` now validates `Σ allocations.amount_inr == total_inr` with ₹1 tolerance (422 with clear message otherwise). `_apply_grant_eligibility` rewrote to a per-head loop when `allocations` is non-empty — each head compared against remaining approved limit (peer invoices fetched ONCE outside the loop for perf), excess added to `ineligible_for_grant_inr` + `over_budget_amount_inr`. Legacy single-`budget_head_code` path preserved for back-compat. **Frontend** (`pages/TournamentOps.jsx`): InvoicesTab now calls `fetchTournamentBudgets({tournament_id, body_id, status:"Approved"})` on mount. New "Budget Head Set-Off" block with add/remove rows, each row's dropdown filtered to that tournament's approved heads (labels + sanctioned limits shown) — already-picked heads excluded from sibling rows. Live sum vs total delta pill ("Balanced ✓" / "Short by ₹X" / "Over by ₹Y"). Save button disabled unless sums match + all heads picked + invoice total > 0. Upload button disabled with tooltip when no approved budget exists for that tournament+body. Invoices table's "Head(s)" column renders each allocation on its own line (`HeadLabel · ₹amt`). AI extraction preserved: pre-fills seed the first allocation if the AI-suggested head exists in the approved budget, else left blank. **Testing** (`iteration_87.json`): 6/6 pytest GREEN in `test_trim_multi_head_invoices.py`, 13/13 frontend acceptance criteria GREEN. Reviewer nits (future): allocation stored via head_label — consider storing budget-head UUID instead so renames don't silently mismatch; enforce backend total > 0.

- **Feb 2026 — Sprint MPCA-KPI-RealData + Tooltips**: **(1)** Fixed the KPI band's "Active Members = 0" bug — `bodies.body_children_activity()` was counting `db.members` only, which is empty for most bodies (their people live in `db.players`). Now sums both: `active_members = COUNT(members, status='Active') + COUNT(players, status='Active')` for each child body. MPCA scope on the preview instance now shows **204 active members across 10 Divisions** (was 0). **(2)** Fiscal cycle for the "Disbursed" tile was hard-coded to `2025-26`; now imports `CURRENT_FISCAL_CYCLE` from `routes.reimbursement_schemes` so it follows the active season (2026-27 shows ₹0 which is correct — no claims disbursed this season yet, whereas 2025-26 still had ₹77k). Season label on the tile continues to follow `useSeason()` on the frontend. **(3)** Added hover tooltip to every KPI tile — new `title` attribute on `KpiTile` component composed from three optional props: `meaning` (one-line "what this metric represents"), `source` (which Mongo collection + filter), and `formula` (the actual aggregation). Rendered via the browser's built-in `title` for zero-dep hover. A small `?` glyph appears in the top-right of any tile with a tooltip so users know it's interactive. Every tile in the current 6-tile band is annotated: Active Members (Σ members + Σ players active), Pending Claims (COUNT status IN [Submitted, Division_Recommended, MPCA_Sanctioned, Returned]), Overdue Tasks (Pending WHERE now − last-approval > SLA-hours), Disbursed (Σ approved_amount_inr from Disbursed claims for the active season), Grant Utilisation ((Σ disbursed) ÷ (Σ annual_grant) × 100), Clean-Slate Bodies (COUNT bodies with 0 overdue). Users can now trace any figure back to its data source in one hover.

- **Feb 2026 — Sprint MPCA-Dashboard-KPIs + Remove-Potential**: **(1)** Removed the "Total Potential (FY 2026-27)" pill from `/schemes` header — the number was a heuristic ceiling based on rate cards, not real disbursement data, and misled office bearers about what MPCA "should" spend. Empty flex slot preserved for future placement. **(2)** Rewired the Dashboard KPI band from 4 stale tiles ("Fiscal cycle 2025-26" hard-coded, "Across N divisions" duplicated) to 6 real-value tiles driven by `/bodies/{code}/children-activity`: **Active Members** (with count of live child bodies), **Pending Claims** (green tone + "Zero backlog · ERP-clean" when zero), **Overdue Tasks** (turns oxblood only when > 0, else "All N divisions on-time"), **Disbursed** (Season · dynamic from `useSeason()`), **Grant Utilisation %** (colour-graded — green ≥ 70%, brass ≥ 30%, oxblood otherwise — shows `₹X of ₹Y`), **Clean-Slate Bodies** (N of N with zero overdue — a first-class ERP-value KPI that grows as bodies stay compliant). Grid moved from `lg:grid-cols-4` to `xl:grid-cols-6` responsive layout. `useSeason` context wired into Dashboard so the sub-label follows the season switcher. Live verification via curl on MPCA scope showed sensible numbers: 4 pending, 12 overdue, ₹77k / ₹3L disbursed = 26% utilisation, 6/10 clean-slate bodies.

- **Feb 2026 — Sprint MPCA-Preview-Robustness**: Three tightly-scoped fixes around document preview + KYC extensibility. **(1) Preview fallback chain**: `DocumentPreview.jsx` now goes image → iframe → unsupported/download, so files without a recognisable URL suffix and without a definitive `Content-Type` header still render correctly for the ~95% case (image or PDF). `<img onError>` flips to iframe mode; `<iframe onError>` bubbles to the "unsupported" fallback screen. State via `imgFailed` + `iframeFailed` refs. **(2) Download always available**: Even when the modal was opened with `hideExport` (e.g. from the review drawer), the "unsupported" fallback screen shows a prominent "Download File" button — because if the user can't preview it, they need at least one way to see it. The download button uses a plain `<a href download>` so browser handles the mime-type resolution. **(3) Add-other-document utility on KYC**: `PlayerDetail.jsx` KYC tab now shows a new `AddOtherDoc` row (dashed cream card) at the bottom of the "Other Documents" section when persona is MPCA/Division. MPCA/Division give the doc a Label (e.g. "Character Certificate") + pick a file → click Add → file lands in `player.documents` with `doc_type=other:{label}`. Backend `POST /players/{pid}/documents` whitelist was tightened but now explicitly ALLOWS any `other:*` prefix as a bypass for free-form docs. Verified via curl: `POST /players/{pid}/documents?doc_type=other:MPCA-Added+Character+Cert&url=/api/uploads/xyz` → HTTP 200, doc persists on player. All lint clean, no console warnings.

- **Feb 2026 — Sprint MPCA-PlayerProfile-Alignment**: The Player Profile after MPCA approval was showing wrong Overview fields (Mother/Siblings/Height/Weight/Domicile/Residency-Since — legacy) and an empty KYC page even though the player had already uploaded every document during registration. Fixed end-to-end: **(1)** Extended `Player` model with `place_of_birth_city`, `place_of_birth_state`, `last_season_division_code`, `bcci_registered`, `bcci_registration_year`, `is_employed` (mirroring the reg form) + `extra_info: Dict[str, str]` for Division/MPCA-added free-form info that was NOT on the reg form. **(2)** `mpca_approve()` now copies all six MPCA-151 fields onto the Player row AND seeds the `documents` list from every registration doc URL (photo, aadhaar, PAN, birth cert, address proof, samagra × 2, consent, no-study affidavit, bonafide, marksheet OR employment triple, NOC previous division, cancelled cheque, GST certificate) plus every `other_docs` entry (each stored with `doc_type=other:{label}`). **(3)** `PATCH /players/{pid}` whitelist extended with the six new fields + `extra_info`. **(4)** Frontend `PlayerDetail.jsx` — Overview tab rewritten: removed Mother, Siblings, Height, Weight, Club/Academy, Proficiency, Domicile, Residency-Since, Address District, Guest Sub-Type, TW3, Employment, Education (all legacy); added a "Place of Birth · Cross-Division · BCCI" grid with the six MPCA-151 fields; added a new "Other Information" section rendering `extra_info` as pills with an inline Add-row form (label + value + Add button). Editable draft prunes down to the reg-form fields only. **(5)** KYC tab (`DocumentsTab`) — `DOC_SLOTS` cut down to the 21 slots that mirror the reg form exactly; removed Class-10, Class-12, Transfer Certificate, Affidavit, Hospital Certificate, Signature Specimen. Added a new "Other Documents (from registration)" grid below the standard slots that renders every `doc_type=other:*` entry with the label preserved. Every existing doc now opens via `<DocumentPreview hideExport>` (inline image / iframed PDF, no download button) instead of an external-tab link. **(6)** Backfill script ran for every existing approved-via-registration player — Mangesh Yadav's KYC page now surfaces all 8 uploaded docs + 1 Other Doc plus his Overview shows Ghosi / Uttar Pradesh / DIV-IND / BCCI 2022. Post-approval KYC is complete without Division/MPCA having to re-upload anything.

- **Feb 2026 — Sprint MPCA-Campaign-Toggle + Preview-Fix**: (1) **Preview modal file-type sniffing**: `/api/uploads/{uuid}` URLs carry no extension so the earlier regex-only check said "Inline preview not supported" for every uploaded file — including plain jpg photos. Fixed by adding a HEAD-request sniffer in `DocumentPreview.jsx`: when neither `isImageByUrl(url)` nor `isPdfByUrl(url)` matches, on modal open we fire a `fetch(url, {method:'HEAD'})` and read the `Content-Type` header. Result cached in local `sniffedType` state (`image` | `pdf` | `other`). Renders a "Loading preview…" pulse until the HEAD returns. Also added an `onError` handler on the `<img>` tag that flips to PDF mode as a last-resort fallback. The final "unsupported" screen now hides the "New Tab / Download" hint when the modal was opened in `hideExport` mode (matches the earlier no-export requirement). (2) **Campaign End/Resume toggle**: MPCA and the owning Division can now flip a campaign from Active → Paused (and back) at any time from `/player-registrations`. New button `[data-testid=pr-toggle-active-{cid}]` sits on every Approved campaign card, styled oxblood ("End Campaign") when active and green ("Resume") when paused, with a confirm() prompt. Under the hood it calls `PATCH /player-registration-campaigns/{cid}` with `{is_active: bool}` — the endpoint already existed, RBAC via `_may_own`. When paused, the public token endpoint returns **HTTP 410 Gone** with message "This campaign is no longer active." — the existing invite tokens also 410 out. Existing registrations against a paused campaign stay untouched and reviewable. Verified via curl end-to-end: create MPCA campaign → 200 on public token → PATCH is_active=false → 410 on public token → PATCH is_active=true → 200 again → 403 when a different Division tries to toggle.

- **Feb 2026 — Sprint MPCA-PlayerReg-Preview**: Two follow-ups to the Player Registration sprint. (1) **Attachments preview-only**: Replaced the anchor-link ATTACHMENTS row in the review drawer with an inline preview grid that renders every doc via `DocumentPreview` — images inline via `<img>`, PDFs via `<iframe>` with `#toolbar=0&navpanes=0`. Added a `hideExport` prop to `DocumentPreview` that suppresses both the "New Tab" and "Download" buttons. All standard MPCA-151 doc slots (photo, aadhaar, pan, birth cert, address proof, samagra × 2, consent, no-study affidavit, bonafide, marksheet OR employment triple, NOC previous division, legacy affidavit / cancelled cheque / GST) render as tiles when their URL is populated; the `REGISTRATION_DOC_SPEC` single source of truth from the earlier sprint is reused so nothing gets missed. Any `other_docs` entries render in a second row of oxblood-toned tiles labelled "Other · {label}". Test IDs: `pr-doc-preview-{field}` and `pr-doc-preview-other-{i}`. (2) **Other Documents editable + AI-analysed**: The public reg form has always accepted a free-form `other_docs` list, but the amend modal previously hid it — MPCA/Division couldn't add or remove. Now the modal has a dedicated "Other Documents" section: existing entries listed with Preview + Remove buttons, plus an add-row (Label + File + "Add Doc" button) that uploads to `/uploads` and immediately persists via `POST /player-registrations/<id>/edit` with `patch: {other_docs: [...]}`. Every add/remove appends an audit event `event=edited` with `diff: {other_docs: [old, new]}`. On the backend, `player_doc_ai.run_full_registration_ai()`'s `doc_slots` list was extended to 22 entries covering every MPCA-151 slot (was only 7 core docs), and it now iterates `player_data.other_docs` and appends `Other · {label}` items so Gemini sees the FULL picture during review — no more missed docs. Verified end-to-end via curl: submit → other_docs=2 persists → MPCA /edit → 3 items + audit event `edited` with diff on other_docs. Cleanup complete, DB stays pristine.

- **Feb 2026 — Sprint MPCA-Season-Schemes**: (1) Fixed Inter-Divisional Host/Visitor scheme bug — the Finance Console was using scheme 2-D for BOTH panels and splitting heads by keyword ("travel", "food" → visitor; else host). Now `tournament.host_scheme_code` and `tournament.visiting_scheme_code` are properly set: Inter-Divisional → host=**2-D** (Hosting), visiting=**2-C** (Travel); Inter-District → host=**2-B** (Hosting), visiting=**2-C** (Travel). `_auto_scheme_for()` in `/app/backend/routes/tournaments.py` now returns a 3-tuple `(main_scheme, host_scheme, visiting_scheme)` and stamps all three on every new tournament. Existing tournaments back-filled via a Mongo script. Finance Console (`TournamentFinanceConsole.jsx`) now loads TWO scheme specs (host + visitor) when they differ, renders TWO input-variables sections labeled with the scheme code, and calls `/schemes/{code}/compute-budget` twice in parallel. `preview.host_preview.head_allocations` drives the left card, `preview.visitor_preview.head_allocations` drives the right — no more keyword split for two-scheme tournaments. Legacy single-scheme tournaments (Inter-School, Inter-Club, camps) still use the keyword fallback so nothing else regresses.
- **Feb 2026 — Sprint MPCA-Season-Schemes**: (2) Season-versioned scheme storage. `db.reimbursement_schemes` is now keyed by composite `(scheme_code, fiscal_cycle)`. Master JSONs live under `/app/backend/data/schemes/{cycle}.json` — currently `2025-26.json` (18 schemes, umpire ₹700 / scorer ₹500 / coach ₹1,500) and `2026-27.json` (33 schemes, umpire ₹1,000 / scorer ₹750 / coach ₹2,000). Same scheme code coexists across seasons with different rate cards; the compute endpoints (`GET /schemes/{code}/input-spec`, `POST /schemes/{code}/compute-budget`) accept `?fiscal_cycle=` and default to `CURRENT_FISCAL_CYCLE`. `list_schemes`, `get_scheme`, and `scheme-for-body` all filter by season; tournaments carry `fiscal_cycle` which the Finance Console passes explicitly on POST compute calls. New endpoint `GET /reimbursement-schemes/seasons` returns `{current, seasons:[{fiscal_cycle, scheme_count}]}` for the Season Switcher.
- **Feb 2026 — Sprint MPCA-Season-Schemes**: (3) Mid-year in-app scheme editor. `PUT /reimbursement-schemes/{code}?fiscal_cycle=` accepts a `SchemeUpdatePayload` (heads, conditions, required_documents, is_active, revision_note) and stamps a fresh `revision_history` entry `{version, changed_at, changed_by, changed_by_body_code, note, changed_fields}` on every save — full audit trail. MPCA-only (403 for Divisions/Districts). Schemes Register (`/schemes`) — Save button now calls PUT, prompts for a mandatory revision note, sends `?fiscal_cycle=season`. Inline heads editor renders a 4-column grid (label · ₹ rate · display text · remove) with an "+ Add budget head" button. Revision History panel below Budget Heads displays every past revision with version, date, editor, note, and list of changed fields. Total Potential card now shows the active season instead of hard-coded "FY 25-26". `POST /reimbursement-schemes/clone-season {from_cycle, to_cycle}` clones every scheme into a new season (409 if target already exists, 404 if source empty) so MPCA can prep next year's rate card. Testing agent found + fixed one bug in clone_season (was copying source `id` UUID → unique-index collision on first clone; now regenerates `uuid.uuid4()` per doc). Backend: 9/9 tests pass, frontend smoke green.

- **Feb 2026 — Sprint MPCA-Schemes-2026-27**: User uploaded `MPCA_Master_Document_2026-27.html` (asset #1) and requested that every scheme in the ERP reflect the revised 2026-27 rates. Rebuilt `/app/backend/data/reimbursement_schemes.json` from scratch by parsing the HTML via `analyze_file_tool` → structured JSON extraction across all schemes (1-A, 1-B, 2-A, 2-B, 2-C, 2-D, 2-E, 2-F, 3-A, 3-B, 3-D, 3-E.1/2/3/4, 4-A/B/C/D/E/F/G, 5-A/B, 6-A/B, 7-A, 9-1 through 9-6 — **33 schemes total**, up from 18). New additions: **2-E** (Inter-Club 'A' Grade tournament reimbursement), **2-F** (renamed from duplicate 2-D — Match-Officials' Match Fees / Conveyance / DA for Inter-Divisional matches), **3-E.1** (District team award — was missing), **4-A/4-B/4-D** (BFHC health-care welfare / State apparels / Multi-day outright victory incentives), **4-E/4-F** (Lifetime Achievement / Nishkaam Karmyogi awards), **5-A** (Ground equipment grant), **9-1 through 9-6** (BCCI hosting fees, match ops, ball specs, venue infra, hospitality, accreditation — split from single legacy 9-BCCI). Every scheme carries `fiscal_cycle: "2026-27"`. Key rate uplifts vs 2025-26 fallback constants in `scheme_calc.py`: **Umpire Fee ₹700 → ₹1,000/day**, **Scorer Fee ₹500 → ₹750/day**, **Coach match-day honorarium ₹1,500 → ₹2,000/day**, **Manager match-day honorarium ₹1,000 → ₹1,250/day**. Compute defaults on `_compute_2A`, `_compute_2B`, `_compute_2D` bumped accordingly so previews stay accurate when DB head codes don't map 1-to-1. Two orphaned legacy codes (`3-C`, `9-BCCI`) explicitly deleted from `db.reimbursement_schemes` so the collection now mirrors the master doc exactly (33 rows). Existing 2025-26 file backed up to `reimbursement_schemes.json.bak_2025-26`. **Reseed**: `POST /api/reimbursement-schemes/reseed` returned `{"seeded":33}`. **Verified via curl**: `POST /api/schemes/2-A/compute-budget` now returns Umpire ₹12,000 + Scorer ₹4,500 for 6-day school tournament (was ₹8,400 + ₹3,000); 2-B Inter-District 8-day matches → Umpire ₹16,000 + Scorer ₹6,000; 2-D 6-day Inter-Divisional hosting → Coach ₹12,000 + Manager ₹7,500. All 33 schemes validated against `ReimbursementScheme` Pydantic model — no schema violations. Every rupee value from the new master document lives on the corresponding head. Non-monetary "conditions" and "required_documents" bulletized for compact display in the Schemes Register.

- **Feb 2026 — Sprint MPCA-EditableBudgetLines**: User requested that auto-drafted budget line items be editable so hosts can tweak the scheme-generated numbers or add custom items before sanction. Body-type-aware RBAC per user spec: **MPCA edits BCCI + Inter_Divisional; Divisions (or Districts) edit every other scope** (Inter_District, Championship, Invitational, Club, School, etc.). Backend: new `PATCH /api/tournament-budgets/{bid}/heads` endpoint in `tournament_budgets.py`. Accepts `{head_allocations: [...], total_ceiling_inr?, edited_by?}`. `_may_edit_heads(tournament, x_body_type)` gates: State-scope → `scope in {BCCI, Inter_Divisional}`; Division/District → `scope NOT in {BCCI, Inter_Divisional}` AND `doc.body_id == x_user_body_code` (own-row only). Allowed statuses: Draft, Returned, Sent_To_Division, Accepted_By_Division, Revision_Requested, Submitted (pre-sanction). Auto-bumps `total_ceiling_inr` when line-item sum exceeds provided ceiling; mirrors `approved_head_allocations` if already Approved. Frontend: `TournamentBudgetsPanel.jsx` gained inline Edit Lines mode. `canEditHeads` mirrors backend RBAC. Clicking `tb-edit-{bid}` opens a 4-column editor (head · amount · notes · remove) with live total, `Add Line Item` button, Cancel + Save. Verified end-to-end via curl: MPCA-editing-Inter_Divisional 200; MPCA-editing-Inter_District 403; Division-editing-own-Inter_District 200; Division-editing-other's-row 403 "You may only edit your own body's budget row". Data-testids: tb-edit-{bid}, tb-edit-form-{bid}, tb-edit-row/head/amt/remove-{bid}-{idx}, tb-edit-add-{bid}, tb-edit-save/cancel-{bid}. Users can now iterate the auto-draft freely before sending / accepting.


- **Feb 2026 — Sprint MPCA-133+ · Match-Officials Workflow Rework (user refinement of Phase 6)**: User pushed back on Phase 6's placement + workflow. Known deferred: notification schema drift (`recipient_id`+`is_read` at insert vs `recipient_role_id`+`read` at list) is app-wide and pre-existing — the assignment / rejection / reminder notifications ARE persisted but won't show in the bell until the schema is unified. Flagged as reviewer follow-up.

- **Feb 2026 — Sprint MPCA-Phase8 · Player Registration Campaign Request/Approval (1 ticket)**: Eighth batch. Verified 100% via testing_agent iteration_80 (11/11 pytest GREEN + full frontend Playwright walk). **MPCA-116** Divisions must now RAISE A REQUEST to open a player-registration campaign — MPCA approves before the public link becomes usable. Each Division sees only its own campaign (scoping was already in place). Backend: `PlayerRegistrationCampaign` gained `request_status: Literal["Pending","Approved","Rejected"] = "Approved"`, `requested_by`, `request_note`, `approved_by`, `approved_at`, `rejection_reason`. `create_campaign` now defaults `request_status="Pending"` when caller is NOT MPCA (`x_user_body_code != "MPCA"` or `x_role_id not in MPCA_ROLES`); MPCA-created campaigns default to `Approved` with `approved_by/approved_at` stamped. Two new endpoints: `POST /player-registration-campaigns/{cid}/approve-request` (MPCA-only; idempotent — returns 200 when already Approved) and `POST /player-registration-campaigns/{cid}/reject-request` (MPCA-only; requires non-empty `reason`; empty → 400). Public token resolver (`GET /api/public/player-registration/token/{token}`) now returns 403 with "This campaign is awaiting MPCA approval and cannot accept submissions yet" when `request_status != "Approved"` — the public submit endpoint reuses this resolver so submissions are blocked automatically. Frontend `PlayerRegistrations.jsx` CampaignsList completely rebuilt: (1) top-right button reads **"Request Campaign"** for Division persona and **"New Campaign"** for MPCA (`isMPCA` check on `persona.body_type`); (2) status pill (`pr-campaign-status-{cid}`) shows Pending / Rejected / Active / Paused / Expired with distinct color families; (3) italic status line beneath the title: "⏳ Awaiting MPCA approval — public link disabled" for Pending, "✗ Rejected: {reason}" (`pr-rejected-reason-{cid}`) for Rejected, "✓ Approved by {name}" for Approved; (4) Copy / Invites / Preview buttons are HIDDEN for non-Approved campaigns; (5) MPCA-only Approve (`pr-approve-req-{cid}`) and Reject (`pr-reject-req-{cid}` with reason prompt) buttons appear on Pending rows. **Testing** (`iteration_80.json`): backend 11/11 pytest GREEN in `test_mpca_116_campaign_requests.py`; frontend full flow covered for MPCA + DIV-IND + DIV-GWL personas; scoping regression confirmed (DIV-IND does not see DIV-GWL campaigns). Reviewer nits (future work): file at 764 lines could be split into `CampaignsList.jsx`/`RegistrationsInbox.jsx`; consider renaming reject_campaign_request's `approved_by/at` to neutral `actioned_by/at` or add dedicated `rejected_by/at`; upgrade `window.prompt` reject dialog to a heritage modal.

- **Feb 2026 — Sprint MPCA-Phase9 · Tournament Calendar Match-Day Paint (1 ticket)**: Sixth batch. Verified 100% frontend via testing_agent iteration_78 (found + fixed one critical schema-mismatch bug during test). **MPCA-132** Tournament Calendar now paints a tournament ONLY on days where an actual `Fixture` exists (via `/api/matches` → `match_date`), not on every day between `start_date` and `end_date`. Rewrote `tournamentsForDate()` in `TournamentCalendarView.jsx` to index fixtures into a `matchesByTid` `Map<tid, {dates:Set<ISO>, byDate:Map<ISO, [fixtures]>}>` and use that as the primary source. Tournaments with fixtures scheduled paint only on those dates and each cell shows `Name (×N)` if multiple matches happen on the same day, with tooltip listing first 3 fixtures ("HomeTeam v AwayTeam @ 10:00"). Tournaments with NO fixtures yet still render as a **placeholder span** with `border-dashed` + `opacity-60 italic` styling and testid `cal-trn-unsched-{tid}` (vs `cal-trn-{tid}` for scheduled) — the Legend row gained an "Unscheduled" chip so users understand the distinction at a glance. Also added a `sort` inside `tournamentsForDate()` so scheduled entries always render before unscheduled entries and cannot be pushed past the `slice(0, 3)` cap. **Critical bug found + fixed by testing agent**: initial implementation read `f.scheduled_date` / `f.scheduled_time`, but backend `TournamentMatch` model uses `match_date` / `start_time` — every tournament was silently falling to the unscheduled fallback, defeating MPCA-132's core purpose. Fix uses `f.match_date || f.scheduled_date` (defensive fallback). **Testing** (`iteration_78.json`): full flow verified on tournament `fb79e6f3-…` (JS Anand Trophy, 2 real fixtures on 2026-08-08 and 2026-08-15) — cells painted only on those dates, gap days blank, unscheduled tournaments still styled with the dashed pill. Note for future work: `TournamentCalendarView.jsx` is now ~325 lines and would benefit from extracting Legend + Undated into sibling components.

Backend: new module in `backend/routes/match_officials.py` adds `STANDARD_MO_FEES_INR` (Umpire ₹700, Scorer ₹500, Referee ₹1,500, Physio ₹1,200) + `STANDARD_MO_DA_INR` (₹500/400/700/400) rate cards, new `TournamentMatchOfficial` model, new collection `tournament_match_officials`, 6 new endpoints (`GET rates/standard`, `GET/POST tournaments/{tid}/match-officials`, `PATCH/DELETE .../{aid}`, `GET .../summary`). `_mpca_only` header guard enforces X-Body-Type=State for every mutating call (403 for Division/District). Summary endpoint returns `paid_by: "MPCA (central pool)"` and per-role breakdown for actuals reporting. Frontend: new `/app/frontend/src/pages/finance/MatchOfficialsPanel.jsx` (200 lines). Panel shows: (1) rate-card strip with 4 role rows for transparency; (2) MPCA-only assign form with official picker filtered by directory role, days input, notes; (3) assignments table with inline days editor (blur-save PATCH) + per-row delete; (4) summary strip showing Fee Total + DA Total + Grand Total + a "Paid by MPCA" pill in italic. Division / District personas see the panel in read-only mode with an info line "Contact MPCA to raise a request." **Bug found + fixed** by testing agent: `MatchOfficialsPanel.jsx` was using `import api from "@/lib/api"` (default) but `@/lib/api` only exposes named exports — this was blocking webpack compile for the whole app. Fixed to `import { api } from "@/lib/api"`. **Additional fix** applied post-testing: made the `useEffect` that auto-shifts Division persona from Pipeline → Budgets **respect** the `?tab=` URL query param, so `?tab=officials` (and any other deep link) now works for all personas, not just MPCA. **Testing** (`iteration_77.json`): backend 12/12 pytest GREEN in `test_mpca_133_match_officials.py`; frontend full flow verified for MPCA / Division / District personas.

New constants `GENDER_OPTIONS` and `AGE_GROUP_OPTIONS` (16 entries). `handleSave` now maps `age_group → age_cap_years` (Open → null, U-19 → 19, etc.) and `gender → is_womens` (Female → true) so backend model stays untouched. Data-testids `trn-gender-select`, `trn-age-group-select`, `trn-max-squad-input`, `trn-medical-check`. **(2) MPCA-105** `max_squad_size` is now editable both at create-time and later via the Tournament Basics panel. Create modal + Basics both expose a checkbox. `SelectionConsole.jsx` pool row now renders an oxblood blinking `MED ✗` badge (testid `med-missing-{pid}`) when `tournament.medical_required && !p.medical_cleared_at`, or a green `MED ✓` badge (`med-ok-{pid}`) when cleared. Zero visual noise when `medical_required=false`. **(4) MPCA-110** Non-match day allowance. Added `non_match_days` input to schemes `2-A` and `2-B` in `scheme_calc.py` INPUT_SPECS. `_compute_2A` appends a "Non-match day allowance (practice / rest)" head at ½× per-day rate (₹5,000 × 0.5 × N days). `_compute_2B` appends a similar head factoring outstation accom + food ½× rate × N days. - **Feb 2026 — Sprint M39z.ii · Inter-District Pool Step District Population Fix**: User-reported P0: "district is not showing in the pool setting step in case of Inter district tournament." Root cause: `TournamentBasicsPanel.jsx` fetched districts using `parent_code = tournament.host_body_id` directly, but on many Inter-District tournaments the `host_body_id` is a specific hosting District code (e.g. `DIST-ALIR-IND`) — not the parent Division (`DIV-IND`). Querying `/api/bodies?body_type=District&parent_code=DIST-ALIR-IND` returned zero (Districts have no District children), so the pool checkbox grid was empty. **Fix** in the `useEffect` at lines 64-95: when `isDistrictScope` is true, the effect now checks whether `host_body_id` starts with `DIST-`; if yes, it fetches `/api/bodies/{host}` to resolve the parent Division and queries with that; otherwise (Division host) it uses `host_body_id` directly. **Tested** (`iteration_72.json`): 100% frontend green — verified on `DIST-ALIR-IND`-hosted 'Gwalior Division Inter-District Championship' (8 districts under DIV-IND appear correctly, including Alirajpur), on `DIV-IND`-hosted 'Bhopal Division Inter-District Championship' (8 districts), and on Inter_Divisional regression (10 MPCA Divisions unchanged). Existing pool headers with `bodyByCode` name lookup keep working after fetch resolves. No backend changes; no data model changes; no data-testid changes.
