- **Feb 2026 — Sprint M39z.h · Legacy Finance route retired + Parent-Division supervises District-hosted tournaments**: Two related fixes off user reports. **BUG 1** — the legacy `TournamentFinanceDetail` page (accessible at `/tournaments/{id}/finance/legacy`) was still rendering the old BUDGET SHEET / INVOICES / EXTRA EXPENSE / BUDGET VS ACTUAL tabs and "Assign Reimbursement Scheme" empty-state. All that surface is now in the new Console. Retired the route: in `App.js` a `LegacyFinanceRedirect` component (`useParams` → absolute `<Navigate to={/tournaments/${id}/finance} replace />`) permanently redirects the old URL to the new console. Also removed the "Open legacy Finance view" link at the bottom of `TournamentFinanceConsole.jsx` (`fc-legacy-link` gone). Fixed dangling links: `TournamentBudgetsPanel.jsx` (Full Finance Screen + per-row Full detail) and `TournamentInvoicesPanel.jsx` (Upload Invoice / DA) now point to `/finance` (was `/finance/legacy`). (Note: my initial `<Navigate to='../finance' replace />` was a React-Router-v6 footgun — `..` resolves relative to the ROUTE, not the URL, so a top-level route sent users to `/finance` → 404 → Landing; testing agent caught and patched.) **BUG 2** — Divisions supervising a child District-hosted tournament couldn't edit. Extended the M39z.g organiser predicate: added `isParentDivOfHostDist` — a `Division` persona whose body_code ends with the same 3-letter suffix as the DIST-*-XYZ host (convention: `DIV-XYZ` supervises `DIST-*-XYZ`). Applied in 4 places for consistency: `TournamentDetail.canEdit`, `TournamentFinanceConsole.isMPCA`, `TournamentFinanceCard` (both `isOrganiser` field + `deriveAction()` internals), and backend `/api/tournaments/{tid}/finance/matrix` scope guard. Frontend green on all in-scope flows (legacy redirect for MPCA + DIV-IND, no old-UI content, organiser rights for parent Division, leak-prevention for unrelated Division). Non-blocking code-review notes for a future sprint: DRY-up the suffix rule into a shared helper (currently in 4 files), tighten `body_code` length check.

- **Feb 2026 — Sprint M39z.g · Host Division = MPCA-equivalent Organiser**: Divisions now get MPCA-role affordances on the tournaments they host (Inter-District, Clubs, School, Pre-Tournament Camp, Vacation Camp, Reciprocal Matches — every type in the "Division-created" catalog). Fixes user report: "Currently divisions are not able to edit the inter district tournaments — you have to give MPCA rights to division in case of Inter district tournaments and tournaments which only division can create." **The single semantic**: `isOrganiser = isState || (persona.body_code === tournament.host_body_id)`. Any surface that previously gated on `isMPCA` / `body_type === 'State'` now uses this widened predicate on tournament-scoped views. **Backend** — `/api/tournaments/{tid}/finance/matrix` scope guard extended: `is_state = state_header OR body_code == 'MPCA' OR body_code == host_body_id`, so the host Division sees every participant row (Districts under them, external teams, etc.) instead of just their own row — needed to prepare + send + sanction budgets. **Frontend** — (1) `TournamentDetail.jsx` `canEdit = isState || isHostBody` (was: State only) — flows through as `canEdit` prop to `TournamentBasicsPanel`, `MatchCalendarPanel`, `TournamentSquadsPanel`, `TournamentReceiptsPanel`, `ClosureLetterPanel`, enabling the ADD district-pool / ADD extra team / ADD ground buttons and every other manage-surface for the host Division. (2) `TournamentFinanceCard.jsx` — `deriveAction()` reworked to take `tournament` arg + compute `organiserLabel = hostBody === 'MPCA' ? 'MPCA' : hostBody`; all hardcoded "MPCA" strings ("Awaiting MPCA to prepare budgets", "MPCA has sent you…", "MPCA will sanction shortly", "MPCA is processing your reimbursement claim", etc.) now interpolate the organiser body. Host Division opening an empty tournament sees "Set input variables & prepare budgets" + Prepare Budgets CTA (was: "Awaiting MPCA…" dead-end). (3) `TournamentFinanceConsole.jsx` — `isMPCA` widened to include host-body so Pipeline tab, Prepare/Send/Sanction buttons, revision-review strip, per-body IV overrides are all visible to host Divisions; persona chip shows "DIV-XXX (organiser)" instead of just "DIV-XXX". (4) **Bonus** — Access-denied card on `TournamentDetail.jsx` (matches the existing pattern on Finance Console) — non-participating personas hitting a tournament GET now see a clean oxblood card instead of the CRA dev-error overlay. **Testing** (`iteration_69.json`): 7/7 backend pytest + 3/3 primary frontend flows + MPCA regression all GREEN. Zero data model changes; zero API contract changes for existing callers; zero regressions on MPCA workflows (all existing MPCA tests pass unchanged). New test file: `/app/backend/tests/test_m39z_g_organiser_rights.py`. Non-host Division on a Division-owned tournament they don't host correctly stays scoped (verified: DIV-BPL on DIV-IND's tournament gets 403, not organiser rights leak).

(2) `MatrixRow.jsx` — the `MatrixRow` (one body's row in the finance status matrix, ~90 lines) and `PoolGroup` (pooled-tournament header + child rows, ~35 lines). - **Feb 2026 — Sprint M39z.e · Correct District-Consolidator scoping to tournament ownership + graceful 403 UI**: Fixed a scoping bug from M39z.d where Districts could see finance flows on MPCA-owned tournaments. Correct model: `tournament.host_body_id` drives everything. **Backend** — `submit_claim` now routes based on tournament host, not persona parent linkage. If tournament host = "MPCA" (Inter-Division): only Divisions can claim (route to MPCA); District submit → HTTP 422 with clear message ("Districts do not participate in MPCA-owned tournaments…"). If tournament host = "DIV-XXX" (Inter-District / Clubs): only Districts under that Division can claim (route to host Division); Districts whose `parent_code` differs from host → 422 with parent-mismatch message. `consolidate` now enforces `t.host_body_id == division_body_id` — wrong Division gets 422 mentioning both codes; consolidation on MPCA-owned tournaments also refused. Tournament GET read-scope for Districts tightened: only tournaments where host = District.parent_code are viewable (no more inheritance from Division participation); anything else returns 403 with "This tournament is not hosted by your parent Division — Districts only participate in Division-owned tournaments." `list_claims` scoping relaxed: when caller passes `route_to_body_id == self`, they're acting as reviewer so body_scope is skipped and the route_to filter provides the visibility (needed so hosting Divisions can see the Incoming District Claims panel data). **Frontend** — `ClaimsPanel` derives `host`, `isDivHosted`, `iAmHostDivision` from `tournament.host_body_id`; description copy branches into 4 states (District/MPCA-hosted-not-applicable · District/Div-hosted-goes-to-Division · Division/hosting-review-and-consolidate · Division/MPCA-hosted-goes-to-MPCA-only). Incoming District Claims panel + consolidate button only render when the current persona is the tournament's host Division. Tab visibility relaxed to include host-Division on their own tournaments so the flow is reachable before any budgets exist. **NEW**: graceful **access-denied card** at the console root — catches 403 from `/tournaments/{tid}` (Districts on MPCA-owned tournaments most commonly) and renders a clear oxblood-bordered card with the backend error message + a "ask MPCA to check your parent Division mapping" hint. No more dev-overlay error banner. Clubs case (Division-owned tournament, no Districts) already covered — Division is the sole claimant, submits their own claim to MPCA, no consolidator needed. **Testing** (`iteration_67.json`): 10/10 pytest cases GREEN in `test_m39ze_host_ownership.py` covering (a) District MPCA-owned 422, (b) District wrong-parent 422, (c) District right-parent → 200 with correct routing, (d) consolidate wrong-Division 422, (e) consolidate MPCA-owned refusal, (f) District GET tournament 403 with right message, (g) District GET tournament 200 for host=parent, (h) list_claims reviewer-scope. Frontend Playwright green across all 4 persona×tournament combos. No regressions; MPCA State-override on approve still works. Lint clean.

- **Feb 2026 — Sprint M39z.d · District → Division → MPCA Consolidator**: Districts now roll up under their parent Division instead of submitting to MPCA directly, so MPCA reviews one master per Division rather than per District. **Backend** — `TournamentReimbursementClaim` model gains `route_to_body_id`, `review_stage`, `parent_claim_id`, `child_claim_ids`, `is_master` fields. `POST /reimbursement-claims/{cid}/submit` now auto-detects the reviewer from the submitting body: District bodies (code starts `DIST-`) route to `bodies.parent_code` (their parent Division) with `review_stage = "Division"`; Division bodies route to `"MPCA"` with `review_stage = "MPCA"`. Notification fanout also switches recipient (district-secretary vs division-secretary vs MPCA-secretary). Approve + Reject now enforce a **route-authority guard** — a wrong Division cannot approve someone else's District claim (403 with clear message); MPCA (State) is the always-allowed override. New endpoints: `GET /reimbursement-claims/consolidator/preview?tournament_id=…&division_body_id=…` returns approved-not-yet-consolidated children + `roll_up_total_inr` + own claim ref; `POST /reimbursement-claims/consolidate` creates or upserts (idempotent per tournament+cycle) a Division master claim with `is_master=true`, `child_claim_ids=[…]`, aggregated head-wise summary (max-of-limits + sum-of-spent + sources[] tag per head), and stamps `parent_claim_id` on every child so they can't be double-consolidated. Master starts in Draft — Division still uploads signed PDF & submits to MPCA using the standard submit endpoint (which now routes to MPCA). `GET /reimbursement-claims` gains `route_to_body_id`, `is_master`, `exclude_consolidated` filters so MPCA can list only masters. `GET /tournaments/{tid}` now allows District viewership when their parent Division is a participant (Districts inherit visibility — needed because they don't have their own participation record but still spend against the Division's team). **Frontend** — `ClaimsPanel` in `TournamentFinanceConsole.jsx` heavily enhanced: (a) District persona sees contextual copy "goes to your Division for review, then rolls up to MPCA as part of the Division master" and their Submit button reads **"Submit to Division"** (not "Submit to MPCA"), (b) Division persona sees a new bold oxblood **"Incoming District Claims"** panel above the main claims list with each pending District row showing Approve / Reject / Open buttons (approve prompts for approved amount = approve-with-variation; reject prompts for reason), (c) once at least one child is Approved a **"Consolidate N · ₹X"** button appears with the roll-up total pre-computed, (d) master claim row shows a MASTER pill next to the claim_ref plus a "+ N District claims inside" hint, child rows show a "ROLLED UP" pill, (e) Submit button auto-relabels per row (Master → "Submit to MPCA", District → "Submit to Division"). Finance Console tabs now render for District personas even when they don't have their own budget row (so they can reach Extras/Invoices/Claims). **Testing** (`iteration_66.json`): 12/12 pytest cases GREEN across submit routing, wrong-Division 403 on both approve+reject, MPCA override, consolidate idempotency + 409/422/403 error paths, parent_claim_id propagation, list-filter accuracy, and tournament read-scope District inheritance. Frontend Playwright green across District / Division / MPCA personas — all data-testids present, all contextual copy verified. New test file `/app/backend/tests/test_m39zd_district_consolidator.py`. No regressions.

- **Feb 2026 — Sprint M39z.c · Extras-Bold + Head-Wise Tally + Inline Claim Draft**: Three user-picked enhancements delivered as one sprint. **(1) Extras Auto-Roll into Budget — VISUALLY BOLD** — the M39w backend logic already re-writes both `head_allocations` and `approved_head_allocations` with the new `"Extra · <label>"` head when MPCA approves an EER. This sprint upgrades the presentation: extra heads now render with (a) `text-mpca-oxblood font-bold` label, (b) a left oxblood-accent bar `border-l-4 border-l-mpca-oxblood`, (c) an oxblood-fill `EXTRA` pill (was a hairline border), (d) a tinted background row `bg-mpca-oxblood/8`. Divisions instantly notice the fresh sanctioned amount they can spend against. **(2) Head-Wise Invoice Tally · LIVE** — new endpoint `GET /api/tournaments/{tid}/spent-by-head?body_id=X` returns per-head `{limit, spent, over, eligible, utilisation_pct}` rolled up across every non-Rejected invoice (Draft + Submitted + Approved) so Divisions see budget consumption LIVE as they upload draft bills. Scope-guarded (Division can only query own body; MPCA any body). `TournamentBudgetsPanel` head-breakdown table rebuilt from 3 columns → **5 columns**: `Head · Sanctioned ₹ · Spent ₹ · Remaining ₹ · Utilised` with a progress bar. Colour coding: `Remaining` is green if positive, oxblood if negative; row is tinted oxblood when spent > sanctioned; totals row shows a red `Over ₹X` pill when any head is over budget. Verified end-to-end via curl: `Accommodation — outstation` head with ₹1,68,000 sanctioned + a ₹1,50,000 Draft invoice → panel showed ₹18,000 remaining, 89% utilised. **(3) Inline Claim Draft** — `ClaimsPanel` in `TournamentFinanceConsole.jsx` completely rebuilt from a status-list to a full inline workflow. New capabilities for Divisions (all inside the console — no legacy-view bounce): (a) **Preview live tally** before drafting — shows `Invoiced ₹ / Eligible ₹ / Over-budget ₹` in a compact strip so the Division decides when to raise the claim. (b) **Start Claim Draft** button — POSTs to `/reimbursement-claims` creating a Draft with tournament + fiscal_cycle + scheme_code pre-filled. (c) **Inline claim row** shows status pill + eligible ₹ + Open detail button. (d) **Draft strip** when the row is Draft/Rejected: guidance copy ("Download the summary PDF from Open detail, get it signed…") + `Upload Signed PDF` file input (accepts application/pdf, uses the standard `/api/uploads` endpoint then calls `/reimbursement-claims/{cid}/signed-pdf`) + `Submit to MPCA` button (disabled until signed PDF exists). (e) **Head-wise Sanctioned/Spent/Eligible/Over table** rendered under every claim row so all stakeholders can audit numbers without opening the detail. Extras rows bolded in oxblood here too; unmatched heads (spend without limit) get a `Unmatched` amber pill. **Verified**: full round-trip via curl — Division creates draft → uploads signed_pdf_url → submits → claim status transitions Draft → Submitted with 11-head summary populated. Screenshot verified all three UX moments: (i) 5-col head-tally table showing 89% utilised Accommodation row, (ii) Extra · Physiotherapist bolded oxblood row after MPCA approve-with-variation, (iii) submitted claim panel with the head-wise summary inline. Lint clean. Test DB rolled back.

- **Feb 2026 — Sprint M39z.b · Post-Sanction UX Fixes (Head-Wise Inline + Invoice CTA + MPCA-Extras Guard + Contrast)**: Four polish fixes off user feedback after the M39z auto-sanction sprint. **(1) Head-wise budget INLINE for Divisions** — `TournamentBudgetsPanel.jsx` was previously a compact one-liner per budget where users had to click "Open" (which looped back to the same page). Rewrote to auto-expand each row (Divisions always; MPCA when status = Approved) with a full head-breakdown table: `Head · Proposed ₹ · Sanctioned ₹` for every allocation, extra-heads pill'd distinctly, and a bold Sanctioned Total footer row showing proposed-vs-sanctioned diff. New `View heads / Hide heads` per-row toggle. **(2) Fixed "Open" loop** — the row-level `Open` link now goes to `/tournaments/:id/finance/legacy` (the full detail with invoice uploaders per head), not back to the console. Renamed to "Full detail". **(3) Prominent Upload Invoice CTA** — `TournamentInvoicesPanel.jsx`: the muted "Upload · Full Screen" text link replaced with a filled oxblood `Upload Invoice / DA →` button (parchment text) so Divisions see the entry point after sanction. Empty-state copy also spells it out: *"No invoices uploaded yet for this tournament (from your body). Click **Upload Invoice / DA** above to add vendor bills or match-official DA forms."* **(4) MPCA-cannot-submit Extras guard** — `TournamentOps.jsx::ExtraExpenseTab` was letting MPCA (State) personas raise/submit their own extra-expense requests because `canRequest` erroneously included `body_type === "State"`. Removed State from `canRequest`; MPCA is now approver-only (Approve · Approve-with-variation · Reject). Backend enforced too: `POST /api/extra-expense-requests` now returns HTTP 403 with the message *"MPCA cannot raise Extra Expense requests — only Divisions and Districts can. MPCA reviews and sanctions (Approve / Approve-with-variation / Reject) the submissions."* when called with `X-Body-Type: State`. **(5) UI contrast polish** — bumped panel headers, footer info tip, budget-ref codes, and empty-state text from muted brass/gray to `text-mpca-charcoal/80` and `font-semibold` on serif titles; STATUS_TONE for `Approved` upgraded to `bg-mpca-green-dark/25 border-mpca-green-dark`. All panel borders bumped from `border-mpca-brass/30` to `border-mpca-brass/40` for sharper edges. **Verified** via screenshots + curl: (a) Division DIV-IND on MY Memorial Trophy sees full 10-head breakdown (Accommodation ₹1,68,000 → Team Prize Runner-up ₹20,000) with Sanctioned Total ₹6,33,100. (b) Invoices tab shows orange "UPLOAD INVOICE / DA →" CTA + clarifying empty-state copy. (c) MPCA on Extras tab has ZERO create/submit buttons; MPCA POST returns 403; Division → submit → MPCA approve-with-variation still works end-to-end (₹5,000 requested → ₹3,000 sanctioned → head added to DIV-IND budget). Lint clean. Test DB rolled back.

- **Feb 2026 — Sprint M39z · Auto-Sanction on Division Accept + Full Finance Tabs for Divisions**: Three P0 fixes off user feedback on the M39u/M39y console. **(1) Auto-sanction on Division acceptance** — the 2-step handoff (Division Accept → MPCA Sanction) added friction with no value since MPCA already authored the ceiling. `POST /api/tournament-budgets/{bid}/division-accept` now transitions **directly to `Approved`** in a single write: copies `total_ceiling_inr → approved_total_inr`, `head_allocations → approved_head_allocations`, stamps both `division_accepted_by/at` AND `sanctioned_by/at`, and appends a single approval-chain step (stage `Approved` · decision `Sanctioned`). Notification fanout to Division ("Budget SANCTIONED · you may now upload invoices and DA/TA claims") + MPCA (record-keeping). Legacy `/sanction` endpoint retained for pre-M39z docs stuck in the transitional `Accepted_By_Division` state. UI updated: `Accept` button relabelled **"Accept & Sanction"**, confirmation prompt explains the auto-sanction upfront, DivisionBudgetCard shows an italic hint *"MPCA authored this budget — accepting sanctions it immediately and unlocks spending."*, batch-actions strip on MPCA view now reads *"N legacy budget(s) still awaiting manual sanction — new acceptances auto-sanction."* State machine docstring in `finance_console.py` updated. **(2) Dedicated Extras tab for Divisions** — `ExtraExpenseTab` extracted from `TournamentOps.jsx` as a named export and mounted as its own tab in the Finance Console (id `extras`, icon `Gavel`). Divisions get "Request Extra Approval" button + full request lifecycle (Draft → Submitted → MPCA Approved/Rejected → head line on the base budget) + tournament expense event log in-place. **(3) Full finance-maintenance tabs restored** — Divisions now see 6 tabs (Budgets · **Extras** · Invoices · DA/TA Forms · Actuals vs Budget · **Reimbursement Claim**); MPCA sees the full 9-tab set (adds Pipeline · MPCA Receipts · Closure Letter). New `ClaimsPanel` component in `TournamentFinanceConsole.jsx` — for Divisions: lists their claim rows for the tournament (or empty state with "Start Claim" button that opens the legacy Finance detail for the sign-and-submit flow); for MPCA: shows claims across bodies with status pill + open action. Wired to `GET /reimbursement-claims?tournament_id=&body_id=` (scoped). Renamed old "Budgets & Extras" tab → simply "Budgets" now that Extras is its own place. **Testing** (`iteration_65.json`): backend pytest 11/11 GREEN (`test_m39z_auto_sanction.py` — state transition + approved-field copy + scope guard + legacy-sanction preserved + revision path still works). Frontend Playwright 100% — 9 tabs render for MPCA persona, 6 for Division, `fc-div-accept-btn` label = "Accept & Sanction", ClaimsPanel renders both empty + populated states, DivisionBudgetCard shows all three state variants correctly. Live curl smoke: DIV-JBP budget for Madhavrao Scindia Trophy transitioned Sent_To_Division → Approved with approved_total ₹1,17,000 in one call.

- **Feb 2026 — Sprint M39y · Division-Side Finance Cleanup (Basics Edit Guard · Empty Pipeline · Head-Wise Budget)**: Three coordinated fixes. **(1) Basics edit guard** — `TournamentDetail.jsx` `canEdit={canEdit || persona?.body_type === "Division"}` was wrongly letting every Division edit any tournament's Basics + Match Calendar. Now uses `persona?.body_code === t.host_body_id` so only the HOST body (plus MPCA) can edit — Inter-Divisional stays MPCA-only, Inter-District becomes host-Division-only, Districts stay locked out of Division tournaments. **(2) Empty Pipeline tab** — the "Pipeline" tab in the Finance Console was always visible but its content was MPCA-only, so Divisions saw a blank space. Now hidden for non-State personas (`show: isMPCA`); Divisions auto-land on the "Budgets & Extras" tab. **(3) Head-wise budget for Divisions + kill "Generate My Budget"** — Divisions were seeing a legacy "Generate My Budget" button that tried to run per-participant IV compute, which we removed in M39w. Deleted the button entirely from `TournamentBudgetsPanel.jsx`. Empty-state copy updated: "MPCA has not sent you a budget yet. You'll be notified in the Action Centre once your budget is ready to accept." Row-level Submit button also hidden for non-MPCA (Divisions never submit; they only Accept via the DivisionBudgetCard). Divisions now click **Open** on the row to see the head-wise breakdown or use the big **Accept Budget / Request Revision** buttons on the sticky card at the top of the console. **Verified via screenshot**: DIV-GWL viewing the Madhavrao Scindia Trophy shows only their own row, no Pipeline tab, Accept Budget CTA prominent, 10-heads row with Open button. Lint clean.

- **Feb 2026 — Sprint M39x · Tournament Acceptance Flow (Action Centre + Prominent Banner)**: When MPCA saves Tournament Basics + participants, participation notifications already fanout via `_notify_participation` in `tournament_participations.py`. This sprint completes the loop by surfacing the pending acceptance to the Division/District UI in three places: (a) **New Action-Centre kind `tournament_acceptance`** — added to `selection_console.tournament_pending_actions` which iterates rows with `acceptance_status == "Pending"` and emits an item pointing to the tournament. Added label + tone in `ActionCenter.jsx` KIND maps. (b) **Prominent orange banner on the Tournament Workspace** — new `myParticipation` state in `TournamentDetail.jsx` fetches this body's participation row via `GET /tournaments/{tid}/participants/{body_code}`; if `acceptance_status === "Pending"`, a full-width orange "ACTION REQUIRED · <body> · Accept your tournament allocation" card appears at the top of the setup grid with Accept + Decline buttons. Accept calls `PATCH /tournaments/{tid}/participants/{body_code}` with `acceptance_status: "Accepted"` and Decline with `"Declined"` + optional reason. After accept, banner replaces itself with a compact green "Accepted as <role> in pool <name> on <date>" strip. (c) **Pending With <body> strip** on the workspace (already existed) — now automatically picks up the new tournament_acceptance items since it reads the same pending-actions endpoint. Email dispatch deferred per user ("we'll do later"). **Verified via screenshot**: Gwalior Division persona opens the Madhavrao Scindia Trophy tournament — sees the orange banner, the pending-with strip and pool details all together; clicking Accept flips the state cleanly. Lint clean.

- **Feb 2026 — Sprint M39w · Finance Process Refactor (Scheme Auto-Map · MPCA Per-Body Head Edit · Extras → Budget Line)**: Five fixes rolling out a cleaner budget flow. **Fix 1 · Scheme auto-mapping** — `_auto_scheme_for()` helper in `tournaments.py` hard-maps tournament_type + scope → default scheme_code (BCCI → 2-D, Inter_District → 2-A, Inter_Divisional → 2-B, MPCA_Championship / Championship → 2-C). Applied automatically when `create_tournament` is called without an explicit scheme_code; MPCA can still override. Fixes the "Inter-Divisional tournament picked Inter-District scheme" complaint. **Fix 2 · MPCA per-body head overrides** — new `per_body_head_overrides: Dict[body_code, Dict[head_name, new_amount_inr]]` on `PreparePayload`. After scheme auto-compute, MPCA can override any head's rupee value for any specific body (not global, not by role); overrides applied in `prepare_budgets` with an audit note "MPCA override ₹X,XXX" appended to the head. Frontend new "Edit heads per body" collapsible under the Prepare button — table of Body × Head editable cells with scheme values shown as placeholders. Visitor-flavour heads only appear editable for Visitor rows; Host row gets all heads. Verified: DIV-CHM Food ₹1,12,000 → ₹1,50,000 override lifted budget total from ₹1,17,000 → ₹1,75,000. **Fix 3 · Division cannot edit** — verified no budget-edit widget in the Division view; only Accept / Request Revision buttons on their own row. **Fix 4 · Extras approval → separate head on per-body budget** — rewrote `extra_expense.approve()`. Was looking up legacy singular `tournament.auto_budget_id`; now targets the correct per-body budget (`tournament_id + body_id + status in [Approved, Accepted_By_Division, Sent_To_Division]`) with legacy fallback. Head is stored as `"Extra · <label>"` so it renders visually distinct from base heads in every UI. Verified: DIV-GWL submitted ₹15k Physiotherapist request → MPCA approved ₹12k → new "Extra · Physiotherapist" head added, budget total ₹3,86,400 → ₹3,98,400. **Fix 5 · Invoices against heads** — already implemented; extras heads work identically because they're stored the same way as base heads. Lint clean throughout. **Screenshot** captured showing the fully new UI: Scheme 2-B badge, Pool A IV editor with 9 fields, live Host + Visitor previews, and the new per-body head-edit table with all 10 Divisions listed as rows.

- **Feb 2026 — Sprint M39v · App-wide Text Contrast Fix**: Root cause of "text not visible" complaints identified — `--mpca-brass` CSS variable was `#e9b949` (light marigold) which yielded only ~1.9:1 contrast against `#f1ead7` parchment (fails WCAG AA badly). **Two-part fix**: (a) Global — darkened `--mpca-brass` to `#b8860b` (dark goldenrod) which passes WCAG AA on parchment (~4.7:1) AND still reads as gold on dark navy backgrounds; introduced `--mpca-brass-light` as `#e9b949` for callers that specifically need the bright marigold on dark bgs (fewer places). Updated both `/app/frontend/src/index.css` and `/app/frontend/tailwind.config.js`. (b) Local — rewrote `TournamentBudgetsPanel.jsx` STATUS_TONE mapping so every status pill uses a dark ink text on a tinted background with a 2px coloured border (was tiny 9px text with 1px border); added the 3 new console statuses (Sent_To_Division, Accepted_By_Division, Revision_Requested) with proper visual weight; bumped pill font-size from 9px to 10px + font-semibold + tracking-wider; replaced underscored labels with spaced text ("SENT TO DIVISION" not "SENT_TO_DIVISION"); "Open" button switched from faded brass-border to solid oxblood-border-2 with hover-fill; budget refs (TB-XXX) switched from `text-mpca-brass` (invisible) to `text-mpca-charcoal/70` (readable); money amounts now `font-semibold text-sm text-mpca-oxblood`. Sidebar accents preserved via `bg-mpca-brass/10` tints which still render fine on dark navy. Restart-required change; frontend restarted cleanly. **Verified via screenshot** — full Budgets & Extras panel now scannable at a glance with all pills, refs, buttons, and totals visible.

- **Feb 2026 — Sprint M39u · Finance Console Tabs (Full Finance Detail in One Place)**: The tournament Finance Console at `/tournaments/:id/finance` now exposes ALL finance detail via a horizontal tab strip below the pipeline matrix. Tabs: **Pipeline** (existing status matrix), **Budgets & Extras** (reuses `TournamentBudgetsPanel` — head-wise allocation, approved-vs-proposed diff, variable items, open-in-detail), **Invoices** (reuses `TournamentInvoicesPanel` — upload PDFs, AI extract, allocate to heads, approve/reject workflow), **DA / TA Forms** (reuses `MatchOfficialDAPanel`), **Actuals vs Budget** (reuses `FinancialSummaryPanel` — auto-rollup Variance, Approved Budget vs Invoices vs Extras vs DA vs Total Spend vs Claim vs Payment vs Outstanding), **MPCA Receipts** (MPCA-only), **Closure Letter** (MPCA-only). All existing panels wired without duplication (import from `@/components/*`). Tab state persisted per session. Legacy Finance page still accessible via footer link at `/tournaments/:id/finance/legacy` for records already in flight. **Verified**: all 7 tabs load without errors (screenshotted Pipeline, Budgets & Extras, Actuals vs Budget). Lint clean.

- **Feb 2026 — Sprint M39t · Consolidated Finance Action Card**: Removed 6 separate finance-related boxes from the Tournament Workspace (Input Variables, Budget & Extras, Invoices + DA Forms, Financial Summary, MPCA Receipts, Closure Letter) and replaced with a **single full-width "Finance · Action for X" card** that computes what THIS user needs to do right now based on: their persona (MPCA/State vs Division/District), the tournament's budget matrix state, and multi-pool status. New component `/app/frontend/src/components/TournamentFinanceCard.jsx` derives priority-ordered actions — for MPCA: revision requests > accepted-awaiting-sanction > drafts-to-send > sent-waiting > claims-to-review; for Divisions: own budget Sent → Accept, Approved → Upload invoices → Submit claim → Awaiting review → Approved → Awaiting disbursement. Card shows action title + one-liner detail + big CTA button + 4-cell mini-stat strip (Proposed / Sanctioned / Spent / Sanctioned bodies X of Y). Tone-coded (oxblood/brass/navy/green/gray) with matching icon. Multi-pool badge shown when applicable. Reuses the existing `GET /finance/matrix` endpoint (already scoped to caller) so Divisions still see only their own numbers on the stat strip. CTA button navigates to the full Finance Console at `/tournaments/:id/finance`. **Verified via screenshots**: (a) MPCA viewing multi-pool tournament with a Chambal revision request → card shows "1 revision request pending · REVIEW REVISIONS", (b) Chambal Division → card shows "Revision requested — awaiting MPCA · VIEW STATUS" in brass tone with only own body's ₹1,17,000 stats, (c) Indore Division (accepted budget) → card shows "Awaiting MPCA final sanction · VIEW STATUS" in navy tone with ₹2,80,800. Setup Boxes grid now contains only 6 items: Basics, Participants, Squads, Match Calendar, Squad Selection, and the wide Finance card + Activity Log + Discussion.

- **Feb 2026 — Sprint M39s · Pool-Aware Finance Console (Multi-Pool Tournaments) + Division Privacy**: Two enhancements in one sprint. **(A) Division Privacy** — `GET /api/tournaments/{tid}/finance/matrix` now reads `X-Body-Code` / `X-Body-Type` headers; MPCA (State) sees all rows, every Division/District sees ONLY their own row and the tournament's IVs are also hidden from non-State callers. Frontend `TournamentFinanceConsole.jsx` hides the entire Status Matrix table when `!isMPCA` — Divisions get only the DivisionBudgetCard with Accept/Request-Revision actions. Response now includes `viewer_scope: "state"|"body"` + `viewer_body_code`. **(B) Multi-Pool support** — full pool-aware flow. Backend: `TournamentBudget` gained `pool_id` + `pool_name` fields; `Tournament` gained `pool_input_variables: Dict[str, Dict]` for per-pool IV overrides. `prepare_budgets` rewrote to iterate pools, compute scheme per-pool with pool-scoped IVs, and create one Host budget per pool + one Visitor budget per (pool, body). `PreparePayload` accepts both `input_variables` (global fallback) and `pool_input_variables` (per-pool). Matrix response now includes `pools[]` summary (pool_id, pool_name, host_body_code/name, member_count, budget/approved totals) and a `multi_pool` boolean. Skip-live and replace-Draft logic scoped per pool_id so re-preparing one pool doesn't disturb another. Frontend: `PreparePanel` renders **pool tabs** (with checkmarks when filled) for multi-pool tournaments, IV editor scoped to the active pool, preview panels labelled with pool name and host body, and a "Prepare All Pools" button requiring every pool to be filled. Status Matrix now renders a `PoolGroup` component per pool with a bold "Pool A · North · HOST: GWALIOR · 5 BODIES · POOL TOTAL: ₹X" header row followed by that pool's member rows. Backward-compat preserved — single-pool tournaments (or ones with `pool_id=null` on participations) render exactly as before via a fallback code path. **Testing**: `prepare-budgets` end-to-end via curl for both single-pool (Bhopal Inter-District, scheme 2-A, 4 districts) and multi-pool (Madhavrao Scindia Trophy, scheme 2-B, 10 divisions across Pool A · North hosted by Gwalior and Pool B · South hosted by Indore) — both produced correct per-pool allocations (multi-pool Pool A = ₹8,54,400 total with different Host allocation ₹3,86,400 due to 2 outstation teams vs Pool B = ₹6,59,200 total, Host ₹2,80,800 with 1 outstation team). Full lifecycle verified: division-accept, request-revision (with reason surfaced on MPCA matrix), and MPCA sanction — all state transitions work. 10 walkthrough screenshots captured (login → Prepare panel → IVs → Send → Division view → Multi-pool prepare tabs → Filled panels → Grouped matrix → Division privacy in multi-pool → Mixed state matrix). Lint clean on both files; backend restarted successfully.

- **Feb 2026 — Sprint M39r · Tournament Finance Console (MPCA-owned budget flow)**: Full redesign of the tournament finance workflow. **Backend**: new module `/app/backend/routes/finance_console.py` with 5 endpoints — `POST /tournaments/{tid}/finance/prepare-budgets` (MPCA one-click: builds one Host budget with full scheme allocation + one Visitor budget per accepted visitor body, using keyword-based head classification travel/DA/food/stay/etc.), `POST /tournaments/{tid}/finance/send-budgets` (flips Drafts to Sent_To_Division in one action, per-body notifications fanned out), `POST /tournament-budgets/{bid}/division-accept` (Division taps Accept), `POST /tournament-budgets/{bid}/request-revision` (Division asks for changes with mandatory reason), `POST /tournament-budgets/{bid}/sanction` (MPCA final approval — this becomes the terminal Approved status that unlocks spending). Also `GET /tournaments/{tid}/finance/matrix` returns a per-body row (Host + Visitors) with budget status, proposed/sanctioned/spent totals, claim status, and a computed `next_action_for` hint {waiting_on, action}. State machine: `Draft → Sent_To_Division → Accepted_By_Division → Approved` with return-path `Sent_To_Division → Revision_Requested → (re-send) → Sent_To_Division`. Extended `TournamentBudgetStatus` Literal in `models.py` with the 3 new states; added audit stamps (prepared_by_name, sent_at, division_accepted_by/at, revision_requested_by/at, revision_reason, sanctioned_by/at, role_flavour). State guards return HTTP 409 on illegal transitions; permission guards return 403 when caller body_code doesn't match target. Prepare is idempotent — skips live budgets (Sent/Accepted/Approved), replaces only Draft/Revision_Requested. **Frontend**: new page `/app/frontend/src/pages/TournamentFinanceConsole.jsx` mounted at `/tournaments/:id/finance` (old page moved to `/tournaments/:id/finance/legacy`). Role-scoped UX — MPCA sees the Prepare Panel (two side-by-side previews for Host vs Visitor budgets, no split logic, single Prepare button) then the Status Matrix with per-row Send/Sanction actions and a batch "Send all Drafts" strip. Division sees `DivisionBudgetCard` at the top with Accept + Request Revision buttons (only actionable when their row is Sent_To_Division). **Action Centre integration**: `/app/backend/routes/selection_console.py` extended — `tournament_pending_actions` emits four new kinds (budget_send, budget_acceptance, budget_sanction, budget_revise) with proper waiting_on routing; `my_pending_inbox` now merges tournaments where the caller is a `tournament_participations` participant (not only host), so participating Districts see their finance actions. Frontend `ActionCenter.jsx` KIND_LABEL/KIND_TONE extended for the 4 new kinds and the Financial stat count includes them. Legacy submit/approve/return/reject endpoints preserved for records already in flight. **Testing** (iteration_64): backend pytest 12/12 pass, frontend 100% — all data-testids resolve, Accept/Sanction click-through both fire and update state, matrix renders four different status rows correctly, permission + state guards work. One reviewer nit noted: sanction endpoint doesn't whitelist head-label names in payload (not exploitable via UI, deferred).

- **Feb 2026 — Sprint M39p · Player Registration AI Deep Validation + Label Visibility Fix**: (A) UI fix — public registration form field labels were rendered in `text-mpca-brass` (#e9b949 marigold) on a `bg-mpca-parchment` (#f1ead7 cream) background, giving unreadable contrast. Bumped to `text-mpca-green-dark` with `font-semibold` at 11px across (i) `Field` labels, (ii) document-upload tile labels, (iii) "Other supporting documents" heading, (iv) `Section` headings now display as serif-oxblood underlined subheads. (B) New backend module `core/player_doc_ai.py` — Gemini 3-Flash-Preview vision extraction across the KYC bundle with deterministic rules engine on top. Checks implemented: (1) Aadhaar number/name/DOB OCR + form-match, (2) Aadhaar duplication guard on submit (blocks with HTTP 409 across all campaigns), (3) age-based PAN mandation (age ≥ 18 forces PAN), (4) PAN OCR pattern check + name match, (5) Birth cert QR extraction + URL-prefix verify against `https://dc.crsorgi.gov.in/crs/` (no live HTTP — pure prefix check per user brief), (6) 3-year marksheet PDF detection (`distinct_academic_years`), (7) Cancelled cheque name + IFSC match, (8) Cross-document name/DOB consistency. Verdict tri-state (Recommend_Approve · Manual_Review · Recommend_Reject) with critical/warning/info trays and overall confidence. (C) Wiring: `POST /api/public/player-registration/submit` fires an async background AI report immediately after insert; new `POST /api/player-registrations/{rid}/ai-full-review` endpoint for reviewers to re-run on demand. New model field `PlayerRegistration.ai_full_report`. (D) Frontend surface: `PlayerRegistrations.jsx` detail view now shows a **Deep AI** button (oxblood-outlined) beside the existing AI Review button, and a full-width colour-coded verdict card with critical/warning/info lists, age computed, PAN-required flag, Aadhaar-duplicate reference, and Gemini model + timestamp footer. **Testing**: unit tests on rules engine — age computation (23/16), name similarity fuzzy (1.0/1.0/0.66/0.0), verdict rollup for clean/bad-QR/2-year-marksheet all correct. API smoke test — Aadhaar dup returns HTTP 409, ai-full-review endpoint stamps report with age=21, pan_required=true, warnings for missing docs, verdict=Recommend_Reject. Screenshot smoke on `/register/player/:token` deferred due to preview CDN issue; lint clean; backend restarted successfully.

- **Feb 2026 — Sprint M39 · Action Center · Activity Log · Discussions**: Three cross-cutting features that make the ERP self-guiding. **(1) Action Center** (`/action-center`) — new persona-scoped page + endpoint `GET /pending-actions/me` that aggregates every pending item across the ERP waiting on the caller. Body-scoped: MPCA state office bearers see items `waiting_on == "MPCA"`; Division/District bodies see items where `waiting_on` matches their body_code or "Division"; Match Officials see their own Draft/Rejected DA forms. Filter chips per action-kind (Squad Draft, Input Variables, Submit for Approval, Budget Approval, Claim Review, DA Fill, Closure Letter, etc.) with counts. Stats cards summarise Total/Squad/Financial/Tournament. Sidebar link added. **(2) Tournament Activity Log** — new setup box `box-activity` on TournamentDetail opens `TournamentActivityLog.jsx` — a chronological timeline that reads `/shared/audit-log?record_id={tid}` and renders each event with module badge, action, actor name/role/body, timestamp, and any structured detail fields. Reuses the existing `write_audit_log` infrastructure. **(3) Discussions** — new `routes/discussions.py` module with two flavours: (a) **Per-tournament thread** — auto-created on first open via `GET /discussions/tournament/{tid}`; new setup box `box-discussion` on TournamentDetail opens a `DiscussionThread` UI inline. (b) **General inbox** at `/discussions` — MPCA ↔ Division/District 1-to-1 threads with `pair_key = sorted([bodyA,bodyB]).join("::")` for stable reuse. RBAC enforced server-side: MPCA can open with any body; a Division/District can only open with MPCA (403 with clear message otherwise). Every message may carry `@mentions[]`; frontend has a live mention picker (`/discussions/mentions/candidates`) that surfaces active postings. Tagged users receive an `add_notification` fanout so mentions show up in their notification bell. Message list scrolls independently, composer supports Cmd/Ctrl+Enter to post. **Testing** — Backend curl: (a) tournament thread auto-create → 200. (b) POST message + list → 200/1 msg. (c) DIV-IND → MPCA inbox → 200. (d) DIV-IND → DIV-BPL inbox → 403 with clear "Divisions and Districts can only communicate with MPCA…" message. (e) `/pending-actions/me` returns `scope=mpca count=28` for Secretary and correctly scoped items for Division. Screenshot smoke test confirmed Action Center renders 28 items + 5 filter chips + 4 stats cards; Discussions renders threads list + composer + working recipient dropdown (65 options for MPCA).

- **Feb 2026 — Sprint M38d-i · Match Officials KYC + Player Registration division + AI · Match Calendar sync · Signed copy fix · Sidebar collapse**: Six-part upgrade covering the whole match-officials + player-registrations experience plus UI polish. **(1) M38d · Signed nomination copy visible to MPCA reviewers**: The `View Signed Copy` chip was nested under `!isMPCA` so state-level reviewers never saw the uploaded PDF — pulled it out so it renders for everyone whenever `squad.signed_copy_url` is present (green pill with tooltip showing upload date + uploader). **(2) M38e · Sidebar collapse + independent scrollers**: `AppLayout.jsx` outer container switched from `min-h-screen` to `h-screen overflow-hidden` so the sidebar and main content each have their own scroll containers (no page-wide scroll). Added a persistable collapse toggle (`sidebar-collapse-btn`, localStorage `sidebar:collapsed`) — sidebar width animates between `w-72` (288px) and `w-16` (64px) icons-only; brand title, persona card, domain headings, nav labels + Coming-Soon block hide gracefully in collapsed mode with hover-tooltips retained. **(3) M38f · Match Calendar ↔ Tournament Calendar sync**: New `GET /api/matches?fiscal_cycle=` endpoint aggregates every `tournament_match` row across all tournaments. `TournamentCalendarPage.jsx` gets a new **Fixtures** view mode toggle (data-testid `cal-view-fixtures`) that lists all individual match rows chronologically (date · time · home vs away · stage · tournament name · venue/ground · result badge) — matches added inside a tournament's Match Calendar panel now show up here immediately. Season-cycle scoped via `useSeason`. **(4) M38g · Match Officials detail + KYC page**: `MatchOfficialBase` extended with 15 KYC/bank/address fields (`date_of_birth`, `gender`, `address`, `city`, `state`, `pincode`, `photo_url`, `pan_no`, `pan_doc_url`, `aadhaar_last4`, `aadhaar_doc_url`, `bank_account_no`, `bank_ifsc`, `bank_name`, `bank_cancelled_cheque_url`, `kyc_status`, `kyc_verified_by`, `kyc_verified_at`, `kyc_notes`). New endpoints: `GET /match-officials/{oid}` (detail fetch), tightened `PATCH /match-officials/{oid}` with 3-way permission — (a) MPCA state office-bearers · (b) owning body admins (Division/District matching `body_id`) · (c) the official themselves matching `X-Persona-Name` (403 otherwise). Self-editors are restricted from changing role / grade / body_id / accreditation / KYC status. New React page `MatchOfficialDetail.jsx` at `/match-officials/:id` — 5-section form (Contact & Basics · Address · KYC Documents · Bank Details · KYC Verification) with photo/PAN/Aadhaar/cheque uploaders, KYC status badge in the header, and disabled/enabled state per permission. `View` link added to each row on the officials list. **(5) M38h · Host Division dropdown on Player Registration form**: Backend `resolve_token` public envelope now returns `divisions[]` (all `body_type=Division` records). `PlayerRegistrationData.preferred_division_code` field added. Public form (`PublicPlayerRegistration.jsx`) renders a `<select>` inside the Contact section (data-testid `pr-pub-host-division`) marked required, listing all divisions. **(6) M38i · MPCA sees Player Registration KYC + AI verdict**: `PlayerRegistration.ai_summary` field added. New endpoint `POST /player-registrations/{rid}/ai-review` runs Gemini via `_run_player_doc_validation` on uploaded KYC docs (photo/Aadhaar/address-proof/birth-cert), maps decision → verdict (CLEAN → Recommend_Approve · FLAGGED/SUSPECTED_FRAUD → Recommend_Reject · else Manual_Review), stamps summary. Review UI in `PlayerRegistrations.jsx` shows an **AI Review** button in the detail header + a colour-coded verdict panel (`pr-ai-summary`, `pr-ai-verdict`) with docs-verified count, confidence, critical issues, advisory notes, timestamp/reviewer. Registration detail also surfaces `preferred_division_code` prominently. **Testing** — Backend curl verified: `GET /api/matches` returns 200, `GET /match-officials/{id}` returns the profile, `PATCH` persists KYC fields (pan_no, kyc_status, address), public envelope returns 10 divisions for the dropdown. Screenshot smoke: sidebar collapse toggle shrinks aside from 288px → 64px, dashboard scrolls independently, icons stay visible.

- **Feb 2026 — Sprint M38c · Fix squad nomination form 404**: The printable nomination form page (`/squads/:id/nomination-form`) was returning `AxiosError · 404 Squad not found` because there was no direct `GET /api/squads/{sid}` endpoint — the frontend was hitting it blindly. Added the endpoint to `routes/selection_console.py::get_squad_by_id` and verified the page now renders cleanly with the MPCA-branded T.A. & D.A. layout, all meta fields, players table, officials block, declaration, and 3 signature blocks. Player-detail sub-fetches (`GET /players/{pid}`) were already fail-safe (`.catch(() => null)`) so any missing player IDs no longer crash the render.

- **Feb 2026 — Sprint M38b · AI verdict badge on Grant Claims list**: Added a compact colour-coded badge next to the status pill on every row in `/grant-claims` — **AI ✓** (green) for `Recommend_Approve`, **AI ?** (amber) for `Manual_Review`, **AI ⚠** (oxblood) for `Recommend_Reject`. Renders only after AI review has been run at least once (`ai_summary` present). Hover tooltip explains the verdict. Test IDs: `claim-row-ai-badge-<id>`. Verified with 3 seeded demo claims covering all 3 verdicts.

- **Feb 2026 — Sprint M38 · AI document-validation for non-tournament Grant Claims**: Extended the Gemini playbook already integrated for tournament docs to the full non-tournament Grant Claim workflow. **(A) Data model** (`routes/grant_claims.py::GrantClaimAiSummary`) — New optional `ai_summary` on `GrantClaim` with `overall_verdict` (Recommend_Approve · Manual_Review · Recommend_Reject), `overall_confidence` (avg of per-doc confidences 0-1), `docs_verified / docs_total`, `amount_match_note`, `critical_issues[]`, `advisory_notes[]`, `validated_at`, `validated_by`. **(B) Backend endpoints** — (1) `POST /grant-claims/{cid}/documents/{doc_id}/re-verify` — manually retry Gemini on a single doc (400 if no file yet). (2) `POST /grant-claims/{cid}/ai-review?actor_name=` — full-claim AI review: (i) re-runs per-doc Gemini on any missing / <0.6 confidence slots, (ii) cross-doc consistency check comparing extracted amounts (best-effort parses "Rs 1,20,000" style strings + max amount across docs) against `claimed_amount_inr` — 5% variance = match ✅, ≤15% = advisory, >15% = critical, (iii) fiscal-cycle date sanity — flags any doc dated outside the claim's fiscal year, (iv) low-confidence signal (<50%) triggers advisory. Rolls up to one verdict: **Recommend_Approve** when all docs verified + avg conf ≥70% + no advisories; **Recommend_Reject** on any critical issue; **Manual_Review** otherwise. Stamps `ai_summary` on the claim. **(C) Frontend** (`pages/GrantClaims.jsx`) — Added an **AI Review** button in the claim action bar (visible to both submitter + reviewer whenever ≥1 file is uploaded), a colour-coded **AI Summary panel** (green/oxblood/brass by verdict) showing `overall_verdict + docs verified + confidence + amount match + critical/advisory bullets + timestamp/reviewer`, and a per-doc **Re-verify** action link next to each Gemini badge. All new UI carries `data-testid` (`ai-review-claim-btn`, `ai-summary-panel`, `ai-verdict`, `ai-amount-note`, `ai-critical-issues`, `ai-advisory-notes`, `doc-reverify-<doc_id>`). **Testing** — Backend curl verified: (1) fresh claim with 11 pre-seeded slots + no files uploaded → ai-review returns `Recommend_Reject` with 11 critical missing-doc issues. (2) Re-verify on empty slot returns HTTP 400 with clear message. (3) Demo claim seeded with mixed AI results → verdict renders as **Manual Review**, avg confidence 73%, amount-match-note surfaces ✓, per-doc re-verify buttons visible on all 3 docs.

- **Feb 2026 — Sprint M37 · Match Officials workflow lockdown**: Delivered all 6 user-requested improvements around Match Officials + DA claims. **(1) Match Officials only see allocated tournaments** — `routes/tournaments.py::_official_visible_tids` filters `GET /api/tournaments` for `X-Body-Type=Official` personas to only tournaments where their name is in `squads.match_officials.{umpire_1|umpire_2|scorer|referee}` OR they have a `match_official_da` row; direct navigation to unallocated tournaments returns HTTP 403. Corresponding `self_create_da_form` guard prevents self-creating a DA for a tournament they're not allocated to. **(2) Squad Officials dropdown** (`SquadDetail::SquadOfficialsSection`) — The 4 on-field slots (umpire_1, umpire_2, scorer, referee) are now `<select>` dropdowns backed by `/api/match-officials` filtered to the slot's role, showing officials from the squad's body + MPCA state-panel (merged & de-duped). Manager/Coach/Trainer/Physio remain free-text. Selecting a name from the DB directly links that persona's login to the tournament (via the M37 Item 4 filter above). **(3) DA workflow rework** — Division approves/rejects DA forms directly (no separate MPCA approval); on approve, notification fires to MO; approved DAs auto-attach to the Division's `Reimbursement Claim` as line items (`da_form_ids[]`) and roll into `summary.da_total_inr` + `summary.da_form_count`. `reject_da_form` sends a warning-severity notification with reason so the MO can revise + re-submit. **(4) DA Review Inbox** — New route `/da-review` + page `DAReview.jsx` — Division/MPCA reviewers see all DA forms in their scope with filter chips (Submitted/Approved/Rejected/Draft/Paid/All), stats cards (Pending · Approved · Rejected · With scheme flags ⚠), inline Approve/Reject actions, plus a drill-down that opens the rich `MatchOfficialDAPanel` in `readOnly + viewerBadges` mode so scheme-breach ⚠ badges appear next to any line that exceeds the scheme rate (DA rate, journey rate, night halt). Sidebar link added under Operations for State/Division/District personas. Backend `list_da_forms` scoped: for Division/District, filters by tournaments hosted in their scope. **(5) Signed Nomination Copy mandate** (Item 6) — Extended `Squad` model with `signed_copy_url + signed_copy_uploaded_at + signed_copy_uploaded_by`. New endpoint `POST /api/squads/{sid}/signed-copy`. `submit_squad_to_mpca` now returns HTTP 400 with clear message ("Signed nomination copy is required · Please download the nomination form, get it signed…") when Division/District tries to submit without an uploaded signed PDF. MPCA host-body drafts are exempt. New printable page `/squads/:id/nomination-form` (`SquadNominationForm.jsx`) — MPCA-branded, includes tournament meta + full squad table (jersey/DoB/role/captain) + 4 team-staff + 4 match-officials + declaration + 3 signature blocks (Selection Chair · Hon. Secretary · Hon. President); tuned for Ctrl+P → Save as PDF. Squad workflow strip now shows 3 conditional buttons for non-MPCA personas: `Download Nomination` → `Upload Signed Copy` → `Submit to MPCA` (disabled until upload). **(6) PapaParse CSV import** — `TournamentWorkspacePanels.jsx::importCsv` swapped naive comma-split for `papaparse` with `header:true, skipEmptyLines:true`, so notes containing commas or quoted fields import correctly. Added `papaparse@5.5.4` to `package.json`. **Testing**: 15/15 backend pytest cases + 6 Playwright E2E flows PASSED (`/app/test_reports/iteration_57.json`). Testing agent created `/app/backend/tests/test_m37_da_workflow.py`. One inline fix by testing agent: `reject_da_form` used `kind='warning'` which isn't a valid `Notification.kind` Literal — changed to `kind='info', severity='warning'`.

- **Feb 2026 — Sprint M36 · Rich DA / TA Form (mirrors MPCA physical form FMPCA 037)**: Replaced the flat 4-field DA form with a full-fidelity replica of the physical **T.A. & D.A. Claim Form**. **(A) Backend model** (`models.py::MatchOfficialDA`) extended with the 8 sections of the physical form: header (name/designation/association/place/purpose auto-filled from `match_officials` profile), `travel_segments[]` (from/to/fare_class/one-way/both-ways/ticket_url), `journey_hours + rate/12hrs`, `days + rate + date_from + date_to`, `conveyance_rate + count`, `incidental_rate + days`, `night_halt_place + amount + hotel_bill_url`, `misc_items[]` (description/amount/receipt_url), and an overflow `attachments[]` bucket. Added `DATravelSegment`, `DAMiscItem`, `DAAttachment`, `DAComplianceFlag` models. **(B) Server-side compute** (`routes/tournament_plan.py::update_da_form`) recomputes ALL derived totals on every PATCH: travel = Σ segments, journey = rate × ceil(hours/12), DA = days × rate, conveyance = rate × count, incidental = rate × days, misc = Σ items; grand total + Indian-numbering-system rupees-in-words helper (`_rupees_in_words`, up to 99,99,99,999). **(C) New endpoint** `POST /api/match-official-da/self-create?tournament_id=&official_name=` — match-official self-creates the DA for a tournament they officiated (idempotent — returns existing draft if present); auto-fills header from `match_officials` collection + tournament data; defaults DA rate from `MATCH_OFFICIAL_DA` grant scheme, journey rate 300, conveyance rate 200. **(D) Compliance snapshot** on submit — `_compute_da_compliance()` compares claimed DA rate vs `MATCH_OFFICIAL_DA` cap, journey rate vs ₹300 standard, night halt vs `MATCH_OFFICIAL_NIGHT_HALT` cap → returns advisory `compliance_flags[]` stamped on the row so MPCA/Division reviewers see ⚠️ badges next to any over-scheme line. Non-blocking — approval still allowed. **(E) Frontend rich panel** `MatchOfficialDAPanel.jsx` — 8 collapsible sections mirroring the physical form; live-computed sub-totals on every field edit; inline uploaders for ticket-per-segment, hotel-bill, misc-receipts, and overflow attachments; Save-Draft + Submit-to-MPCA/Division CTAs; readonly locked when status ≠ Draft/Rejected; scheme-breach ⚠️ pill on `NumField` when `viewerBadges` prop enabled. **(F) TournamentDetail visibility** — match-official persona now sees only 3 setup boxes: **Tournament Basics · Match Calendar · My DA / TA Form** (all 9 financial/squad boxes hidden — Participants Matrix, Squads, Input Variables, Squad Selection, Budget & Extras, Invoices+DA Forms, Financial Summary, MPCA Receipts, Closure Letter). Squads section below the box grid also hidden. **(G) `MyDAForms.jsx` rewritten** — now a lightweight list of the official's DA forms across tournaments; click any row → inline detail using the same `MatchOfficialDAPanel` (single source of truth). **(H) Seed**: Chandrakant Pandit added to `match_officials` collection so the persona has a live profile. **Verified via curl** (5 flows): self-create returns pre-filled header; PATCH with the physical-form example yields exact ₹16,060 total + "Sixteen Thousand Sixty Rupees Only" in words; submit stamps 0 flags when in-scheme; submit stamps 2 flags when DA rate 3500 + journey rate 500 (both over cap); self-create is idempotent. Screenshot smoke test with match-official persona confirmed: 3 boxes visible (basics + calendar + my-da), 9 financial boxes hidden, squads section hidden, DA form panel renders all 8 sections with live totals matching physical form.

- **Feb 2026 — Sprint M35a · One-Submission-Per-Email guard on Public Registration links**: Per user request, tightened the public player-registration link so the same email cannot be submitted more than once on the same campaign. **(A) Backend** (`routes/player_registrations.py`) — `public_submit` now (1) requires `email` (400 if missing), (2) normalises to lower-cased `email_key` and stores it alongside the registration row, (3) rejects duplicates with **HTTP 409** when a `Submitted` or `Approved` row already exists for the same `campaign_id + email_key`. `Rejected` / `Returned` rows do NOT block, so a returned applicant can still re-submit. New non-unique index `(campaign_id, email_key)` for lookup speed. **(B) Frontend** (`PublicPlayerRegistration.jsx`) — email field is now `required`, a persistent amber banner reads `Note · One registration per email on this link. Please double-check your email before submitting.`, and 409/400 errors surface as an inline oxblood banner (`pr-pub-submit-err`) that also scrolls to the top so the applicant sees the exact reason. **Verified via curl** (5 cases): first submit → 200, duplicate → 409, uppercase duplicate → 409 (normalisation), missing email → 400, different email → 200. Screenshot smoke test confirmed banner + `required` email field render on the public route.

Panel confirms ₹2,80,800 default for scheme 2-B, auto-recompute on input change verified. Progress derivation: `progress-step-budget_created` and `progress-step-budget_approved` correctly reflect budget lifecycle.

- **Feb 2026 — Sprint M15 · 7-Bug Fix Pass (verified 100%/100% by testing_agent iteration_30)**: (1) **Tournament scoping tightened** — `_tournament_scope_query` in `routes/tournaments.py` no longer broadcasts MPCA/BCCI-hosted tournaments to Divisions/Districts; Division now sees ONLY own DIV code + child `DIST-*-{SUFFIX}` + tournaments where their body appears in `acceptance.required_from`. Verified: DIV-IND persona sees 1 tournament (Vijay Merchant Trophy), MPCA sees 33. (2) **Squad approval RBAC hardened** — `SelectionConsole.jsx` split condition into `body_type !== "State"` for Submit button and `body_type === "State"` for AI-Review/Approve/Reject; Division users can no longer approve/reject their own submissions. (3) **Match Officials dropdown fixed** — SelectionConsole now merges MPCA state-panel officials with the persona-body's officials via two parallel API calls + dedupe by id; Umesh Bharadwaj (State Panel Umpire) now appears in the dropdown alongside body-specific officials. (4) **Scheme-Aware Budget Calculator** — new `/app/backend/routes/scheme_calc.py` with deterministic formulae for **2-A / 2-B / 2-C / 2-D / 3-A / 3-D** driven by user-provided input variables (`match_days`, `rooms_visiting`, `outstation_teams`, `team_strength`, `rail_fare_per_pax`, `camp_days` etc.). Two new endpoints: `GET /api/schemes/{code}/input-spec` (returns variables + defaults + hints + select-options) and `POST /api/schemes/{code}/compute-budget` (returns computed `head_allocations` with `formula` field like "₹1,800 × 20 rooms × 6 days"). Frontend `TournamentFinanceDetail.jsx` scheme picker is now a **2-step wizard**: Step 1 filters to tournament-related schemes; Step 2 renders the input form with live-recompute-on-change (300ms debounce) and previews computed budget totals inline. Verified: 2-D with defaults yields ₹2,89,340 across 9 heads; 2-B with defaults across 9 heads. (5) **Overall AI Verdict** — `squad_ai.py::squad_recommendation` now emits `overall_verdict` (PASS / PASS_WITH_REMARKS / FAIL) + `verdict_reason` + `critical_issues` array. Logic: PASS if no critical issues; PASS_WITH_REMARKS only if the single issue is a small KYC gap (≤5); FAIL otherwise. Prominent 2-line verdict banner rendered at the top of `/squads/:sid/review` with colour-coded border (green/brass/oxblood). (6) **Grant Claim Submit RBAC** — `canSubmit` now gated with `!isMPCA` — MPCA Secretary can no longer see the Submit button on any grant claim, only Approve/Reject. Fixed regression that surfaced after previous test. (7) **Tournament-schemes are read-only in Schemes Master** — `SchemesMaster.jsx` new `TOURNAMENT_SCHEME_CODES` set (2-A / 2-B / 2-C / 2-D / 2-E / 3-C / 3-D / 9-BCCI) drives an `isTournamentScheme` predicate that hides the "Claim under this scheme" button and shows an inline read-only notice pointing the user to the Tournament Reimbursement Matrix. Also: fixed pre-existing AI-doc-verification `NoneType` bug (upload-record path lookup was using `storage_path` instead of `_path`).

New `/app/backend/routes/grant_claims.py` with: (1) `GrantClaim` model with pre-seeded document slots matching scheme's required_documents; (2) full workflow endpoints `POST /api/grant-claims` (idempotency: rejects duplicate active claims per body+scheme+cycle), `POST /api/grant-claims/{cid}/document/{doc_id}` (attach file + auto-run AI verification), `POST /api/grant-claims/{cid}/submit` (validates all docs uploaded), `/approve`, `/reject`; (3) **AI Document Verifier** (`_ai_verify_document`) — Gemini 2.5-flash with FileContentWithMimeType inspects each uploaded doc against its expected `required_label`, returns `{matches, confidence 0-1, document_type_detected, key_details, issues, verdict_note}`, all persisted onto the document slot; (4) **AI Eligibility Recommender** `GET /api/schemes-recommendations` — filters schemes by body_type, computes total potential (sum of head rates), marks already-claimed vs not-started, returns tailored `recommendation_note`; (5) **AI Assistant Chat** `POST /api/ai-assistant/chat` — session-based Gemini chat that receives (a) the caller's body + type from scope headers, (b) their existing claims for FY 2025-26, (c) full filtered schemes catalogue as system context, so the assistant can answer "which grants am I eligible for?" or "documents for Scheme 1-A?" with scheme-specific citations. Never invents schemes. Live-tested with Indore Division — returns 3 top schemes (1-A, 6-A, 6-B) with document list; (6) `PATCH /api/reimbursement-schemes/{code}` — MPCA-only scheme editor. **Frontend**: three new pages + one floating panel — **`/schemes`** (`SchemesMaster.jsx`) shows 21 schemes with filter chips (Annual_Grant / Reimbursement / Camp / Award / Welfare / Infrastructure / Revenue_Share), 2-column layout, sticky detail pane with editable heads/documents/conditions for MPCA (Edit button surfaces inline text fields with add/remove), and "Claim under this scheme" CTA for Div/Dist that routes to `/grant-claims/new?scheme=1-A`; **`/grant-claims`** (`GrantClaims.jsx`) 2-column claim list + document upload panel where each required doc slot is a card with Upload button → AI badge (green "AI verified · 92% confidence" or oxblood "AI flag · {reason}") plus AI-extracted document_type_detected label, plus Submit/Approve/Reject actions; **AI Assistant floating FAB** (`AIAssistantPanel.jsx`) — bottom-right pill button visible only to `division-secretary` and `district-secretary` personas, opens a 400×560px chat panel with sticky suggestion chips ("What grants am I eligible for?" · "Documents for Scheme 1-A?" · "How to claim Ground Maintenance?"), auto-scroll, session-based conversation. Sidebar rearranged — Financial group now leads with "MPCA Schemes Register" and "Grant Claims", followed by Tournament Reimbursement Matrix + Reimbursement Claims. Read-side body-scoping applied to `grant-claims` — Divisions see own + child Districts' claims, MPCA sees all. Verified via curl: DIV-IND potential ₹59.3L across all applicable schemes, MPCA can edit any scheme, Gemini chat replies with scheme_code citations.

- **Feb 2026 — Sprint M13-A · Backend Read-Side Scoping + Sprint M13-B · Camps Module + Sprint M13-C · AI Squad Recommendation**: Massive multi-part sprint delivering data-isolation, coaching camps, and an AI-powered squad review workflow. **(A) Backend Read-Side Scoping** — new `/app/backend/core/scoping.py` with `get_scope(request)` reading X-Persona-Id / X-Body-Code / X-Body-Type / X-Persona-Name headers and `body_scope(scope, field)` returning MongoDB query fragments (own DIV code + child DIST-*-{suffix} regex for Division, exact DIST code for District, `{}` for State). Endpoints: `GET /api/squads/{sid}/recommendation` (deterministic verdict), `POST /api/squads/{sid}/ai-second-opinion` (Gemini 2.5-flash via Emergent LLM key — returns 3-4 crisp observations in ≤100 words), `POST /api/squads/{sid}/notify-ai-review` (in-app notification to MPCA Secretary on Division submit). Frontend `/squads/:sid/review` page: 4-stat scorecard strip (Quality Score / Overlap % / KYC Gaps / Body Spread with colour-coding — green ≥70, brass 50-70, oxblood <50), AI Observations panel, side-by-side Selected (oxblood header) vs AI-Recommended XV (green header) — matched players get `CheckCircle2` green highlight, missed recommendations get "MISSED" tag, KYC gaps show inline in oxblood, role-bucket score reasoning, "Ask AI for Second Opinion" button that streams Gemini's verdict inline. Role Balance card shows batters/all-rounders/keepers/pace/spin counts (zeros flagged in oxblood). Body Spread card renders a bar chart of every contributing body with over-70% warning banner. Selection Console's Submit-to-MPCA flow now auto-calls `/notify-ai-review` and displays "AI Review" button (Sparkles icon) beside Approve/Reject when the squad reaches `Awaiting_MPCA_Approval`. **Live tested** with Indore Division XV squad on CT Sarwate Trophy — Quality 96.7/100, Overlap 40%, KYC Gaps 45, Bias 100% DIV-IND, Gemini opinion identified all 4 issues autonomously.

- **Feb 2026 — Sprint T-RIM · Tournament Reimbursement Matrix (Assembled from MPCA Master Document)**: Full per-tournament finance console linked to the Tournament module. New models: `ReimbursementScheme` (heads + conditions + fiscal_cycle), `InvoiceHeadAllocation` (one invoice can span multiple budget heads via allocations array), `TournamentReimbursementClaim` (Draft → Submitted → Under_Review → Approved/Rejected with auto-generated summary sheet snapshot). New endpoints: `GET /api/reimbursement-schemes[/{code}]`, `POST /api/reimbursement-schemes/reseed`; `GET /api/reimbursement-claims[/{cid}]`, `POST /api/reimbursement-claims` (idempotent — 409 on duplicate active claim per tournament+body+cycle), `POST /api/reimbursement-claims/{cid}/{submit,start-review,approve,reject,comment}` (approve requires `approved_amount_inr ≤ eligible`; reject requires notes), `GET /api/tournaments/{tid}/reimbursement-preview?body_id=` (live summary sheet), `GET /api/reimbursement-claims-stats/summary`, and a new `PATCH /api/tournaments/{tid}` to attach `scheme_code`. `TournamentInvoiceBase` extended with `allocations: List[InvoiceHeadAllocation]`; the budget-tracker (`/api/tournament-budgets/{bid}/tracker`) rewritten to compute `spent_inr` per head from allocations first (label match preferred) and fall back to legacy `budget_head_code` — no double-counting. `_compute_summary` builds head-wise Budget/Spent/Eligible/Over rows aggregated across all Approved invoices + approved extra-expense requests.
